  • sms_name <= 18 characters so NotificationTemplatesTest.confirmationFitsOneGsm7SegmentForLongestSeededNames holds for every seed
  • code is immutable
  • documents_needed is never empty and contains no NULL element (services_documents_needed_count)
BranchService Catalogue join entity: the fact that a branch offers a service, and how many concurrent seats it has for it.…

Catalogue join entity: the fact that a branch offers a service, and how many concurrent seats it has for it. A seat is one dedicated service desk (consultant) for that service; this is a stated assumption, and a branch-wide consultant pool is the documented production change. Its row is the pessimistic lock target of the booking transaction and the target of the composite FK on appointments. Catalogue owns the row and never writes it at runtime; the FOR UPDATE is issued by booking's BookingLocks adapter, because locking for a booking is a booking concern. The code reads the row as ServiceOffer (catalogue.domain), which carries the service's id, code, SMS name and duration beside the capacity.

FieldTypeNotes
branchIdUUIDFK branches.id; PK part
serviceIdUUIDFK services.id; PK part
capacityshortCHECK between 1 and 20; concurrent seats; seeds: POL 1 for every service, SAN FICA 3 CREDIT 3 CARD 2 others 2, all other branches 2

Invariants

  • PK (branch_id, service_id); a row exists iff the branch offers the service (GQE has no HOME row -> 422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH and no slots)
  • At any instant the active appointments for (branch, service) overlapping it number <= capacity; enforced by appointments_seat_no_overlap, made deterministic by SELECT ... FOR UPDATE on this row
  • capacity never decreases below the number of active seats in use (not enforced in v1: reference data is immutable at runtime)
  • Production move to a branch-wide pool: EXCLUDE (branch_id WITH =, seat_no WITH =, tstzrange WITH &&) with the lock on the branches row; services of different durations then share seats, which a unique index on starts_at could never express
PublicHoliday Catalogue entity: one gazetted (Schedule 1) or proclaimed (s2A) public holiday date. Only scheduled dates are…

Catalogue entity: one gazetted (Schedule 1) or proclaimed (s2A) public holiday date. Only scheduled dates are stored; PublicHolidayCalendar derives the observed Monday after a Sunday holiday under Act 36 of 1994 s2(1) as a pure function.

FieldTypeNotes
holidayDateLocalDate (date)PK
nameString (varchar 80)e.g. 'National Women\'s Day'
sourceenum-as-textCHECK IN ('SCHEDULE_1','PROCLAMATION'); 4 Nov 2026 election day is PROCLAMATION

Invariants

  • Derived Mondays (2026-08-10, 2027-03-22, 2027-12-27) are never stored; PublicHolidayCalendar.isHoliday(d) = d in table OR (d is Monday AND d-1 in table) (PublicHolidayCalendarTest.sundayHoliday9Aug2026IsObservedOnMonday10Aug, sundayHolidays21Mar2027And26Dec2027AreObservedOnTheFollowingMonday)
  • Saturdays get no substitute (PublicHolidayCalendarTest.saturdayHoliday21Mar2026GetsNoSubstitute)
  • Branches with open_on_public_holidays=false (all seeds) have no slots on a scheduled or derived holiday; GET /branches/{code} reports them as closedDates with reason PUBLIC_HOLIDAY and the name
  • Coverage is explicit: PublicHolidayCalendar.coveredThrough() is 31 Dec of the last seeded year (2027-12-31); HolidayCoverageCheck logs a WARN at start-up and every day at 06:00 SAST from 60 days before the booking window reaches it, and again once the window has passed it (from 2027-12-03), and the fix is a new migration, V9 now that V8 holds the lookup limit's counts, never a code change (PublicHolidayCalendarTest.coverageEndsOnTheLastDayOfTheLastYearItHolds)
Appointment Booking aggregate root and the only JPA entity in the system. Customer fields are embedded (no Customer…

Booking aggregate root and the only JPA entity in the system. Customer fields are embedded (no Customer entity, POPIA s10). Carries the AppointmentStatus state machine, @Version for cancel races, and the value objects AppointmentReference, MobileNumber and CustomerName. Inserted only by BookAppointmentHandler and never updated; deleted by CancelAppointmentHandler when it is cancelled (V6), and by RetentionPurge once it has ended, where retention is switched on; the appointments table is read and written only by the booking module, which also implements scheduling's BookedIntervalsPort.

FieldTypeNotes
idUUIDPK; generated by the application
referenceAppointmentReference (varchar 13)APT-XXXX-XXXX, UNIQUE, CHECK regex pins the 13 characters; the customer-facing identifier; never changes
branchIdUUIDComposite FK (branch_id, service_id) -> branch_services
serviceIdUUIDComposite FK with branchId
seatNoshort1..capacity; lowest free seat chosen under the branch_services lock; CHECK seat_no BETWEEN 1 AND 20
startsAtInstant (timestamptz)UTC instant; rendered as OffsetDateTime at Branch.timezone (+02:00)
endsAtInstant (timestamptz)= startsAt + service.durationMinutes, computed server-side; CHECK ends_at > starts_at
statusAppointmentStatus (varchar 12)CHECK IN the five states; default CONFIRMED on insert
customerFirstNameCustomerName (varchar 60)NFC-normalised, trimmed, letters/marks/space/hyphen/apostrophe; CHECK trimmed and non-empty
customerLastNameCustomerName (varchar 60)As above
customerMobileMobileNumber (varchar 16)E.164 only, CHECK ^\+27(6[0-9]|7[0-9]|8[1-5])[0-9]{7}$ (same constant as MobileNumber.E164_PATTERN); the customer identity for the overlap and per-service rules and the key of the per-mobile advisory lock; rendered masked 082 *** 0101 only to a caller who proved the number, redacted 082 *** **** everywhere else
customerEmailString (varchar 254), nullableCollected only if the Tier 3 email channel ships (otherwise always NULL); optional; lower-cased; presence queues an EMAIL message
cancelledAtInstant, nullableSet by cancel(now), in memory: the cancel deletes the row, so it reaches the response, never the table (V6); CHECK (status = 'CANCELLED') = (cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)
checkedInAtInstant, nullableSet by checkIn(now); domain-only transition in v1; CHECK set iff status IN ('CHECKED_IN','COMPLETED')
completedAtInstant, nullableSet by complete(now); domain-only; CHECK set iff status = 'COMPLETED'
noShowAtInstant, nullableSet by markNoShow(now); domain-only; CHECK set iff status = 'NO_SHOW'
versionInteger (@Version)Optimistic lock; null until the first persist so Spring Data calls persist, never merge (no extra SELECT for an application-assigned UUID); column integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0; the cancel locks and deletes the row only WHERE id and version match, so a cancel that finds it gone or changed gets OptimisticLockingFailureException -> 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND, counted as a wrong try
createdAtInstantSet from the injected Clock by the domain factory (DEFAULT now() is a raw-insert convenience only)
updatedAtInstantSet from the Clock by the factory and by every state change

Invariants

  • ends_at = starts_at + duration (AppointmentTest.endsAtIsStartPlusServiceDuration); the request's endsAt is never read
  • starts_at passed SlotCalendar.validate at booking time: inside the 30-date window, open day, inside hours, on grid, >= now + PT2H
  • No two active appointments share (branch, service, seat) over overlapping [starts_at, ends_at) - appointments_seat_no_overlap
  • No customer_mobile holds two active appointments over overlapping time - appointments_customer_no_overlap
  • At most one upcoming active appointment (ends_at > now) per (customer_mobile, service), checked under the per-mobile advisory lock; past rows never count, because a partial index cannot express 'upcoming' (index predicates must be immutable and now() is not)
  • status changes only through AppointmentStatus.canTransitionTo guards; COMPLETED, CANCELLED, NO_SHOW are terminal (AppointmentStatusTest.terminalStatesRejectEveryTransition)
  • A CONFIRMED appointment whose time has passed stays CONFIRMED in v1 (no staff actor, no sweeper): canCancel is false, it never blocks a new booking, and its seat lies in the past. Where branchbook.retention.enabled is on, as on the hosted demo, it is deleted with its messages and idempotency keys within the hour after it ends, whatever its status
  • Every insert writes exactly one CONFIRMATION SMS row (plus EMAIL when customer_email is present, which needs the Tier 3 email channel) in the same transaction, PENDING, or FAILED with RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP where the number has reached the daily cap; cancel deletes every CONFIRMATION row, whatever its status, writes a CANCELLATION row likewise (a capped one is reported in the response but never stored) and then deletes the appointment, so no cancelled appointment is kept
  • Appointment status never depends on NotificationMessage status
  • reference and customer_mobile are immutable; a reference collision re-runs the booking transaction once
  • Persisted with EntityManager.persist and flushed immediately at step 8, so every constraint violation surfaces at that line and the JdbcClient statements that follow see the row
IdempotencyKey Booking entity: the record that one POST /api/v1/appointments attempt, keyed by the client's Idempotency-Key…

Booking entity: the record that one POST /api/v1/appointments attempt, keyed by the client's Idempotency-Key UUID, produced a committed booking. Claimed as the first statement of the booking transaction with INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE ... WHERE the existing row has expired, RETURNING key, so concurrent duplicates serialise on the primary key and an expired key is taken over. Written and read only through JdbcClient, by JdbcIdempotencyKeyStore behind booking's IdempotencyKeyStore port; the IdempotencyKey record itself is only the parsed header, a canonical UUID. It stores no response snapshot and no name or number, only an unkeyed SHA-256 of the details, so it is pseudonymised, not anonymous: a replay re-renders the current AppointmentResponse from appointment_id. A cancel deletes the appointment but keeps the key, with no appointment, until it expires, so a retry of that booking is refused rather than booked again (V6).

FieldTypeNotes
keyUUIDPK = Idempotency-Key header (400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED if missing or not a UUID)
requestHashString (varchar 64)Lower-case SHA-256 hex (CHECK ^[0-9a-f]{64}$) of the parsed details, one per line: branch code, service code, startsAt (UTC instant), mobileE164, firstName, lastName (BookAppointmentCommand.requestHash), names after CustomerName normalisation; unkeyed, so a guess of the details can be confirmed against it: pseudonymised, not anonymous; kept after a cancel deletes the booking, and purged within 25 h
appointmentIdUUID, nullableFK appointments.id, ON DELETE SET NULL (V6); set by step 10 of the booking transaction, and set to null when a cancel deletes the appointment
responseStatusshort, nullableAlways 201 once filled (CHECK); set together with appointmentId at step 10 and kept when a cancel nulls it, so 201 with no appointment marks a cancelled booking; an appointment id implies 201 (CHECK idempotency_keys_completed_consistent, since V6)
createdAtInstantBound :now from the Clock
expiresAtInstant:now + 24 h; from then the claim takes the key over, and the hourly purge (IdempotencyKeyPurgeJob) deletes rows WHERE expires_at <= :now

Invariants

  • A row exists only for a committed 201: any 4xx/5xx rolls it back (IdempotencyIT.failedBookingLeavesNoKeyRow)
  • Same key + same request_hash -> 201 replayed with Idempotent-Replayed: true, body re-rendered from the current appointment; once a cancel has deleted it, the same key and hash is 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED and never books again (IdempotencyIT.sameKeyAfterItsBookingIsCancelledReturns422AndNeverBooksAgain); same key + different hash -> 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED
  • Two concurrent requests with the same key: the second INSERT ... ON CONFLICT waits on the first's uncommitted row; when it returns nothing, a new READ COMMITTED statement reads the committed row, so one books and the other replays the same reference (IdempotencyIT.concurrentSameKey_bookOnceAndBothReceiveSameReference)
  • A key is honoured for exactly 24 h, whether or not the hourly purge has removed it: after that the claim takes it over and the same key is a new request (IdempotencyKeyPurgeJobIT.keyIsHonouredForTwentyFourHoursThenBooksAnewEvenBeforeThePurge)
  • The table holds no names, mobile or email; only the hash, the appointment id and the status. The hash is unkeyed, so the row is pseudonymised: someone who guesses the details can confirm them against it
NotificationMessage Notification outbox row: one customer-facing message (SMS or EMAIL) for one appointment event, written inside…

Notification outbox row: one customer-facing message (SMS or EMAIL) for one appointment event, written inside the booking or cancel transaction by NotificationOutbox and delivered by NotificationDispatcher through the MessageGateway port. A domain object mapped by a JdbcClient row mapper (not a JPA entity): every write is an explicit statement (insert, claim, mark, supersede, retry, delete), so nothing depends on deferred dirty checking.

FieldTypeNotes
idUUIDPK; also the client reference sent to the gateway so at-least-once delivery is deduplicable
appointmentIdUUID, nullableFK appointments.id, ON DELETE SET NULL (V6); null only for a CANCELLATION whose appointment the cancel has deleted (CHECK notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only)
kindenum-as-textCHECK IN ('CONFIRMATION','CANCELLATION')
channelenum-as-textCHECK IN ('SMS','EMAIL')
recipientString (varchar 254)E.164 or email; never logged; rendered as recipientMasked: 082 *** 0101 inside an AppointmentResponse (the caller proved the number), 082 *** **** / n***@example.com in the inbox and everywhere else
subjectString (varchar 160), nullableEMAIL only (CHECK set iff channel = 'EMAIL')
bodyString (text)SMS body <= 160 GSM-7 septets (NotificationTemplates + Gsm7 are the authority; the CHECK is a coarse 320-character guard); email plain text
statusNotificationStatus (varchar 8)CHECK IN ('PENDING','SENT','FAILED'); default PENDING
attemptsshort0..5; incremented per send attempt
nextAttemptAtInstantBound :now at insert; :now + 2/4/8/16 s after failures 1-4; drives the partial dispatch index
lastErrorString (varchar 500), nullableA short code: the gateway's refusal code such as SIMULATED_FAILURE (cut to 500 by MessageGateway.Refused), GATEWAY_ERROR when the gateway threw, an AWS code built from the exception's type and typed reason, such as AWS_THROTTLED, never AWS's message, or MARK_FAILED; operator text only, never PII. The stable reason is the companion field failureReason (column failure_reason): DELIVERY_FAILED, SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION, RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP (where the daily cap is on) or DISPATCH_ERROR, set exactly when status is FAILED
failureReasonFailureReason (varchar 32), nullableDELIVERY_FAILED, SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION, RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP (where the daily cap is on) or DISPATCH_ERROR. Set exactly when status is FAILED; the UI picks its copy from this code.
providerMessageIdString (varchar 64), nullable'sim-' + UUID from the simulated gateway, or AWS's MessageId from AwsSmsGateway; MessageGateway.Accepted refuses an id that is not 1 to 64 characters (MessageGatewayTest.providerIdMustFitItsColumn); placeholder for provider callbacks
correlationIdString (varchar 64)Copied from the booking request's X-Correlation-Id (MDC); 'sys-' + UUID for work outside a request such as DemoDataLoader
createdAtInstantBound :now at insert
sentAtInstant, nullableCHECK (status = 'SENT') = (sent_at IS NOT NULL)

Invariants

  • Written only by direct call inside the caller's transaction (never AFTER_COMMIT, never a Spring event); the confirmation goes through booking's ConfirmationOutbox port with Propagation.MANDATORY, so it refuses to run outside a transaction (NotificationOutboxIT.refusesToWriteOutsideATransaction); a booking that fails after the SMS is written leaves no row (BookAppointmentIT.failureAfterTheSmsIsWrittenRollsTheSmsBackWithTheBooking)
  • PENDING -> SENT, or PENDING -> PENDING (attempts+1, backoff 2/4/8/16 s), or PENDING -> FAILED on the fifth failed attempt or on a refusal the gateway calls permanent (failure_reason DELIVERY_FAILED), when the dispatcher finds a confirmation's appointment still kept as CANCELLED (SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION; only one cancelled before V6, or by hand), or when marking the row itself failed (DISPATCH_ERROR); a CANCELLATION is deleted where it would be marked SENT or FAILED; or a CONFIRMATION written FAILED at once with RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP past the daily cap, never sent, while a capped CANCELLATION is never stored; FAILED -> PENDING only via POST /api/v1/messages/{id}/retry (attempts reset to 0, failure_reason cleared; built in M6, RetryPolicy) or the runbook's guarded SQL, only for DELIVERY_FAILED or DISPATCH_ERROR, and never for a CONFIRMATION whose appointment is CANCELLED or has started
  • Claimed one row per transaction with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED; claim, send and mark share that transaction (NotificationDispatcherIT.twoDispatchersNeverSendTheSameMessageTwice); a crash re-sends at most the one message in flight
  • A CONFIRMATION is never delivered after its appointment's cancellation: the cancel deletes it, waiting for one being sent (NotificationDispatcherIT.cancelDeletesThePendingConfirmationAndTheSentCancellationIsDeleted, NotificationDispatcherIT.confirmationInFlightWhenTheCancelArrivesIsSentBeforeTheCancellation), and none outlives its appointment (notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only)
  • Body never contains the full mobile number, its last four digits or any identifier beyond the reference
AppointmentReference Value object (booking/domain): the customer-facing booking identifier APT-XXXX-XXXX.

Value object (booking/domain): the customer-facing booking identifier APT-XXXX-XXXX.

FieldTypeNotes
valueString (13 chars)'APT-' + 4 + '-' + 4 characters from the 31-character alphabet ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 drawn with SecureRandom; stored uppercase in varchar(13)

Invariants

  • Matches ^APT-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}$ (AppointmentReferenceTest.usesOnlyUnambiguousAlphabet)
  • parse(input) trims, upper-cases and inserts missing hyphens before matching; anything else is rejected without touching the database (AppointmentReferenceTest.normalisesLowercaseAndMissingHyphensOnLookup)
  • Uniqueness is the DB constraint appointments_reference_key; a collision re-runs the booking transaction once (BookAppointmentIT.referenceCollisionRerunsTransactionOnce)
  • Prefix is always APT, never a real bank's format
  • Never appears in a problem body or a log line: no 409 reveals another appointment's reference, logs name an appointment by its id, and toString() prints APT-****-**** (AppointmentReferenceTest.toStringNeverShowsTheReference)
MobileNumber Value object (booking/domain): a South African mobile number normalised to E.164. parse succeeds only when…

Value object (booking/domain): a South African mobile number normalised to E.164. parse succeeds only when libphonenumber (region ZA) reports type MOBILE and the E.164 form matches E164_PATTERN, the same constant the V2 CHECK uses, so a parsed number can never violate the database CHECK. Two renderings leave the domain: masked() for a caller who has just proved the number, redacted() for everyone else.

FieldTypeNotes
e164String^\+27(6[0-9]|7[0-9]|8[1-5])[0-9]{7}$, e.g. +27825550101; 080, 086, 087, 088 and 089 are excluded even where libphonenumber reports a sub-range as MOBILE
display()String (derived)'082 555 0101'
masked()String (derived)'082 *** 0101'; only inside an AppointmentResponse returned to a caller who supplied the full number (POST 201, replay) or its last four digits (GET, cancel)
redacted()String (derived)'082 *** ****'; the inbox (GET /api/v1/messages recipientMasked); logs carry no recipient at all and never the reference (they name a message by its messageId), and the MDC holds only the correlation id
last4()String (derived)'0101'; compared with X-Mobile-Last4 in constant time; the lookup second factor, so it is never printed next to a reference outside a proved response
isPublishedDemoNumber()boolean (derived)True for the published demo block, 082 555 0000 to 0199 (+27825550000 to +27825550199). Those numbers always go through the simulated gateway, are never counted by the daily cap, and are the only ones taken where branchbook.booking.demo-numbers-only is on (else 422 DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED)

Invariants

  • parse accepts '082 555 0101', '0825550101', '+27 82 555 0101', '+27 (0)82 555 0101', '27825550101', '0027825550101' and yields +27825550101 (MobileNumberTest.sixInputFormatsNormaliseToPlus27825550101)
  • Rejects landlines 01-05, VoIP 087, 080/086 and any non-MOBILE type with MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED; the VoIP fixture is one libphonenumber reports as VOIP and the test asserts that type (MobileNumberTest.rejectsLandline021Voip087AndTollFree080)
  • E164_PATTERN equals the appointments_customer_mobile_e164 CHECK read from pg_constraint (SchemaConstraintIT.mobileCheckMatchesMobileNumberPattern)
  • Raw input is never persisted or logged; toString() returns redacted()
CustomerName Value object (booking/domain): a first or last name as the customer types it on any keyboard, normalised once…

Value object (booking/domain): a first or last name as the customer types it on any keyboard, normalised once so that validation, the request hash and storage agree.

FieldTypeNotes
valueString (1-60 code points)NFC-normalised; U+2019, U+2018 and U+02BC mapped to the ASCII apostrophe (iOS Smart Punctuation); trimmed; internal whitespace collapsed; ^\p{L}[\p{L}\p{M} '\-]*$

Invariants

  • Accepts D'Oliveira typed as D’Oliveira and decomposed accents such as Müller (CustomerNameTest.acceptsCurlyApostropheFromIos, acceptsCombiningDiaeresis)
  • Rejects digits, control characters, CR/LF and punctuation other than hyphen and apostrophe, so nothing injectable reaches templates or headers
  • Length counted in code points after normalisation, matching PostgreSQL varchar(60) character semantics
  • Names appear only in the email greeting and in proved AppointmentResponses; never in SMS bodies, logs or problem bodies
Slot Read model (scheduling/domain), never persisted: one bookable start for (branch, service, date) produced by…

Read model (scheduling/domain), never persisted: one bookable start for (branch, service, date) produced by SlotCalendar in Branch.timezone with an injected Clock. Advisory only; the booking transaction revalidates. Active intervals come from booking's implementation of scheduling's BookedIntervalsPort, so scheduling never reads the appointments table itself.

FieldTypeNotes
startsAtInstantopens_at + k x duration; the API renders it with the branch offset (+02:00) through TimeFormat.isoOffset, one explicit ISO_OFFSET_DATE_TIME formatter at Branch.timezone, independent of Jackson 2/3 time-zone features
endsAtInstantstartsAt + duration; <= closes_at
seatsAvailableintcapacity minus the distinct seats that active appointments hold during [startsAt, endsAt), never below 0; a full slot stays listed with 0, which the web app renders as 'Full' (1 as '1 left')

Invariants

  • Produced only for days with an OperatingHours row that are not public holidays (unless openOnPublicHolidays) within the half-open window [today, today + P30D): exactly 30 bookable dates, today through today + 29, in Branch.timezone (BookingWindowTest.thirtyDayHorizonGivesExactlyThirtyBookableDates)
  • startsAt >= now + PT2H (slots inside the lead time are simply absent)
  • SlotCalendar.validate(startsAt) returns OK or exactly one code, by fixed precedence DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW > SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY > SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS > SLOT_OFF_GRID > SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME, and is the function the booking transaction calls at step 3 (BookAppointmentHandler); it agrees with the slots availability lists and keeps that precedence (SlotCalendarTest.validateAgreesWithGeneratedSlotsForEverySeededHoursPatternAndDuration; SlotCalendarTest.validatePrecedence)
  • The window boundaries (bookableFrom, bookableTo) are computed by the server from the Clock and published on GET /branches/{code}; the client never computes 'today'
AppointmentStatus Enum (booking/domain) with canTransitionTo(target). ACTIVE = {CONFIRMED, CHECKED_IN} mirrors the…

Enum (booking/domain) with canTransitionTo(target). ACTIVE = {CONFIRMED, CHECKED_IN} mirrors the partial-constraint predicate; isFinal() is true for COMPLETED, CANCELLED and NO_SHOW. The constant ACTIVE_SQL, ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN'), is the only spelling of the predicate in application SQL.

FieldTypeNotes
CONFIRMEDvalue-> CANCELLED, CHECKED_IN, NO_SHOW
CHECKED_INvalue-> COMPLETED
COMPLETEDvalueterminal
CANCELLEDvalueterminal
NO_SHOWvalueterminal

Invariants

  • canTransitionTo returns true only for the four edges above (AppointmentStatusTest, parameterised over all 25 pairs)
  • AppointmentStatus.ACTIVE equals the WHERE predicate of both EXCLUDE constraints (pg_constraint) and of the partial indexes ix_appointments_slot_lookup and ix_appointments_customer_active (pg_index.indpred) (SchemaConstraintIT.activeStatusesMatchSchemaPredicate)
  • Every native query (lowest free seat, booked intervals, customer pre-checks) builds its predicate from ACTIVE_SQL
  • Only CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED is reachable through the API (ADR-0004)
NotificationStatus Enum (notification/domain) for the outbox row lifecycle.

Enum (notification/domain) for the outbox row lifecycle.

FieldTypeNotes
PENDINGvalueAwaiting a send attempt at next_attempt_at
SENTvalueGateway accepted; sent_at and provider_message_id set
FAILEDvalueFive attempts failed, or one refusal the gateway calls permanent (failure_reason DELIVERY_FAILED, last gateway error kept), or superseded by the appointment's cancellation (failure_reason SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION, only for an appointment kept as CANCELLED, before V6 or by hand), or the mark itself failed after a send (failure_reason DISPATCH_ERROR, last_error 'MARK_FAILED'), or written past the daily cap (failure_reason RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP, never sent); replayable via the retry endpoint unless it is a CONFIRMATION of a cancelled or started appointment. A CANCELLATION is never kept FAILED: it is deleted instead

Invariants

  • PENDING -> SENT | FAILED; FAILED -> PENDING via retry only; SENT is final. A CANCELLATION leaves PENDING by being deleted, once it is sent or has failed for good
  • Never set to SENT before a send was attempted (NotificationDispatcherIT.marksSentWithProviderMessageId)
  • No new status value is needed for supersession: FAILED plus failure_reason SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION keeps the canonical three states

Relationships

ServiceBranchService1 : 0..*A service may be offered at several branches (HOME is absent at GQE)

AppointmentNotificationMessage0..1 : 1..*One SMS (+ one EMAIL if email given) per booking, plus the same per cancellation; FK notification_messages.appointment_id, ON DELETE SET NULL (V6); a cancel deletes the appointment's CONFIRMATION rows, whatever their status, and its CANCELLATION outlives it with no appointment until it is sent or has failed for good

DesignBuilt

Data model

Status. Built: all ten tables, with 61 named constraints: the eight of V2 with the seeds in migrations V1 to V4, V5's index for the per-recipient daily cap, which V7 drops, V6's foreign keys that let a cancel delete its booking, the daily cap's ledger sms_recipient_sends (V7) and the lookup limit's counts lookup_attempts (V8); the booking's runtime SQL from M3; the dispatcher's claim and marks from M5; in M6, the supersede, the retry's lock and reset, and the retention purge's locked batch deletes; and since V6, the cancel's version-checked lock and its deletes of the confirmations and the appointment. The hosted demo runs the same migrations on RDS for PostgreSQL 17.11 in Cape Town.

PostgreSQL 17 (postgres:17-alpine pinned to the exact patch tag current on scaffold day) with CREATE EXTENSION btree_gist. Flyway versioned SQL owns the schema (V1__enable_btree_gist.sql, V2__schema.sql, V3__reference_data.sql, V4__public_holidays_2026_2027.sql, V5__notification_messages_recipient_index.sql, V6__cancel_deletes_the_booking.sql, V7__sms_recipient_sends.sql, V8__lookup_attempts.sql); spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto=validate, which covers exactly one JPA entity (Appointment -> appointments): catalogue tables, idempotency_keys, notification_messages, sms_recipient_sends and lookup_attempts are read and written through JdbcClient, so validate never has to reconcile composite keys, arrays or check-only columns; every instant timestamptz (entities and records use Instant); enums as varchar + CHECK; fixed-length identifiers as varchar + CHECK, never char(n); every constraint named because the names are the ConstraintViolationTranslator contract (ConstraintNamesIT.everyMappedConstraintNameExistsInSchema reads pg_constraint); application SQL never calls now() (every time-dependent statement binds :now from the injected Clock; DEFAULT now() exists for seeds and raw inserts only; NativeSqlUsesBoundNowTest.noJavaSqlLiteralCallsNow scans every string literal in the main sources); no H2 anywhere; every integration test runs on one shared static Testcontainers PostgreSQL 17 via @ServiceConnection. Log hygiene is part of the data model because constraint errors carry key values: the server runs with -c log_error_verbosity=terse -c log_min_error_statement=panic (compose command and Testcontainers withCommand), so DETAIL lines such as 'Key (customer_mobile, ...)=(+27..., ...)' never reach the database log; the datasource sets logServerErrorDetail=false, so the same detail never reaches exception messages; ConstraintViolationTranslator reads PSQLException.getServerErrorMessage().getConstraint() and getSQLState(), never parses message text. One application role in the demo (split owner/app roles are a production note). Table ownership: catalogue owns branches, branch_operating_hours, services, branch_services, public_holidays (read-only at runtime); booking owns appointments, idempotency_keys and lookup_attempts and takes the branch_services row lock through its BookingLocks port; notification owns notification_messages and sms_recipient_sends.

Indexes and constraints

  • appointments_pkey (id)
  • appointments_reference_key UNIQUE (reference)
  • appointments_seat_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (see notes) - gist index, partial on active statuses
  • appointments_customer_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (see notes) - gist index, partial on active statuses
  • ix_appointments_customer_active (customer_mobile, service_id) WHERE status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN') - non-unique on purpose: serves the DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT and CUSTOMER_OVERLAP pre-checks under the per-mobile lock (replaces v1's ux_appointments_customer_service, which could not be time-bounded)
  • ix_appointments_slot_lookup (branch_id, service_id, starts_at) WHERE status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN') - availability counts and seat selection

Notes

Exact DDL (V1__enable_btree_gist.sql, then V2__schema.sql):
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;
-- The invariant of the whole system. '[)' ranges let 09:00-09:30 and 09:30-10:00 coexist; the partial predicate releases seats of CANCELLED/COMPLETED/NO_SHOW rows without a delete. Why EXCLUDE and not UNIQUE (branch_id, service_id, seat_no, starts_at): (1) the customer rule must compare ranges across services of different durations, which only an overlap operator can do; (2) the production move to a branch-wide consultant pool (EXCLUDE (branch_id WITH =, seat_no WITH =, range WITH &&), lock on the branches row) makes unequal grids share seats; (3) a per-service unique index is correct only while every slot sits on one grid, which no constraint enforces and a later change to a branch's opening hours would silently break.
ALTER TABLE appointments ADD CONSTRAINT appointments_seat_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (branch_id WITH =, service_id WITH =, seat_no WITH =, tstzrange(starts_at, ends_at, '[)') WITH &&) WHERE (status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN'));
ALTER TABLE appointments ADD CONSTRAINT appointments_customer_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (customer_mobile WITH =, tstzrange(starts_at, ends_at, '[)') WITH &&) WHERE (status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN'));
-- One upcoming appointment per mobile per service is time-relative; index predicates must be immutable and now() is not, so the rule is checked under pg_advisory_xact_lock and this index only makes the check a lookup.
CREATE INDEX ix_appointments_customer_active ON appointments (customer_mobile, service_id) WHERE status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN');
CONSTRAINT appointments_branch_service_fkey FOREIGN KEY (branch_id, service_id) REFERENCES branch_services (branch_id, service_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_appointments_slot_lookup ON appointments (branch_id, service_id, starts_at) WHERE status IN ('CONFIRMED','CHECKED_IN');
Steps 4 and 5, run before the branch_services lock: SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(:mobileE164, 0)); then SELECT 1 FROM appointments WHERE customer_mobile = :m AND service_id = :s AND status IN ACTIVE_SQL AND ends_at > :now LIMIT 1 (hit -> 409 DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT); then SELECT 1 FROM appointments WHERE customer_mobile = :m AND status IN ACTIVE_SQL AND tstzrange(starts_at, ends_at, '[)') && tstzrange(:startsAt, :endsAt, '[)') LIMIT 1 (hit -> 409 CUSTOMER_OVERLAP). Each statement takes a fresh READ COMMITTED snapshot after the lock wait, so it sees every booking by that mobile that committed before the lock was granted.
Lowest-free-seat query run under the branch_services FOR UPDATE lock: SELECT s.seat FROM generate_series(1, :capacity) AS s(seat) WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM appointments a WHERE a.branch_id = :branchId AND a.service_id = :serviceId AND a.seat_no = s.seat AND a.status IN ACTIVE_SQL AND a.starts_at < :endsAt AND a.ends_at > :startsAt AND a.starts_at > :earliestStart) ORDER BY s.seat LIMIT 1, where :earliestStart is :startsAt less 120 minutes, the longest appointment the schema allows, so the query reads a short range of ix_appointments_slot_lookup, not every past appointment. Customer fields are embedded (no customers table); PII is masked or redacted at the API boundary. Ownership: only the booking module reads or writes this table; scheduling receives active intervals through booking's implementation of BookedIntervalsPort. Appointment is the one JPA entity: inserted with EntityManager.persist and flushed at step 8, and never updated: the cancel detaches it, locks the row with SELECT id FROM appointments WHERE id = :id AND version = :version FOR UPDATE and deletes it with DELETE FROM appointments WHERE id = :id AND version = :version (V6); branch_id/service_id stay UUID columns, never object graphs.
idempotency_keys 6 columns
ColumnTypeConstraints
keyuuidPRIMARY KEY (idempotency_keys_pkey) = Idempotency-Key header
request_hashvarchar(64)NOT NULL, CHECK (request_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') (idempotency_keys_request_hash_format); SHA-256 hex of the normalised branch, service, startsAt instant, mobileE164, firstName and lastName, one per line (BookAppointmentCommand.requestHash); unkeyed, so a guess of the details can be confirmed against it: pseudonymised, not anonymous
appointment_iduuidNULL, REFERENCES appointments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL (idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey, re-created by V6), so a completed key outlives the appointment a cancel deletes
response_statussmallintNULL, CHECK (response_status IS NULL OR response_status = 201) (idempotency_keys_response_status_check), CHECK (appointment_id IS NULL OR response_status IS NOT NULL) (idempotency_keys_completed_consistent, re-created by V6; V2 required both or neither): an appointment id implies a completed key, and 201 with no appointment marks a booking a cancel deleted
created_attimestamptzNOT NULL; bound :now
expires_attimestamptzNOT NULL, CHECK (expires_at > created_at) (idempotency_keys_expiry_after_creation); bound :now + interval '24 hours'

Indexes and constraints

  • idempotency_keys_pkey (key)
  • ix_idempotency_keys_expires (expires_at) - hourly purge DELETE ... WHERE expires_at <= :now (IdempotencyKeyPurgeJob)

Notes

Shape of the DDL (V2__schema.sql names every constraint): CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (key uuid PRIMARY KEY, request_hash varchar(64) NOT NULL CHECK (request_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'), appointment_id uuid REFERENCES appointments(id), response_status smallint CHECK (response_status IS NULL OR response_status = 201), created_at timestamptz NOT NULL, expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL, CHECK ((appointment_id IS NULL) = (response_status IS NULL)), CHECK (expires_at > created_at)); CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_keys_expires ON idempotency_keys (expires_at). v1's response_body jsonb is dropped: it duplicated the customer's names in a second table, made the replay a stale snapshot, and put JSON serialisation inside the lock window. First statement of the booking transaction: INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (key, request_hash, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (:key, :hash, :now, :expiresAt) ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET request_hash = EXCLUDED.request_hash, appointment_id = NULL, response_status = NULL, created_at = EXCLUDED.created_at, expires_at = EXCLUDED.expires_at WHERE idempotency_keys.expires_at <= EXCLUDED.created_at RETURNING key, with :expiresAt = :now + 24 h. A key whose 24 hours have run out is taken over in the same statement and books anew, whether or not the hourly purge has removed it. No row returned -> SELECT request_hash, appointment_id, response_status FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = :key (this SELECT never blocks: the INSERT ... ON CONFLICT is what waited on a concurrent uncommitted row, and this new READ COMMITTED statement sees the committed result): different hash -> 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED; same hash -> load the appointment and replay 201 + Location with Idempotent-Replayed: true, or, where response_status is 201 but appointment_id is null because a cancel deleted the booking, 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED with a fixed detail, so it never books again. V6 re-creates idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey with ON DELETE SET NULL and idempotency_keys_completed_consistent as CHECK (appointment_id IS NULL OR response_status IS NOT NULL). Step 10 completes the row: UPDATE idempotency_keys SET appointment_id = :id, response_status = 201 WHERE key = :key. A 409/422/503 rolls the row back. Written and read only through JdbcClient; owned by booking.
notification_messages 16 columns
ColumnTypeConstraints
iduuidPRIMARY KEY (notification_messages_pkey)

attemptssmallintNOT NULL DEFAULT 0, CHECK (attempts BETWEEN 0 AND 5) (notification_messages_attempts_check)
next_attempt_attimestamptzNOT NULL DEFAULT now(); the application always binds :now (the default serves raw inserts only)
last_errorvarchar(500)NULL; cut to 500 by MessageGateway.Refused; operator text only (the gateway's refusal code such as SIMULATED_FAILURE, GATEWAY_ERROR when the gateway threw, or MARK_FAILED). Companion column failure_reason varchar(32) NULL, CHECK (failure_reason IN ('DELIVERY_FAILED','SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION','RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP','DISPATCH_ERROR')) (notification_messages_failure_reason_check) and CHECK ((status = 'FAILED') = (failure_reason IS NOT NULL)) (notification_messages_failure_reason_consistent): the stable code the API exposes as failureReason
failure_reasonvarchar(32)NULL; CHECK (failure_reason IN ('DELIVERY_FAILED','SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION','RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP','DISPATCH_ERROR')) (notification_messages_failure_reason_check); CHECK ((status = 'FAILED') = (failure_reason IS NOT NULL)) (notification_messages_failure_reason_consistent). The stable code the API exposes as failureReason.
provider_message_idvarchar(64)NULL; 1 to 64 characters, enforced by MessageGateway.Accepted before the mark
correlation_idvarchar(64)NOT NULL; request X-Correlation-Id, or 'sys-' + UUID outside a request
created_attimestamptzNOT NULL DEFAULT now(); the application binds :now
sent_attimestamptzNULL, CHECK ((status = 'SENT') = (sent_at IS NOT NULL)) (notification_messages_sent_at_consistent)

Indexes and constraints

  • notification_messages_pkey (id)
  • ix_notification_messages_due (next_attempt_at) WHERE status = 'PENDING' - dispatcher claim
  • ix_notification_messages_appointment (appointment_id) - notifications[] in AppointmentResponse and the cancel's DELETE of the confirmations
  • ix_notification_messages_created (created_at DESC) - GET /api/v1/messages newest first
  • ix_notification_messages_recipient (recipient, created_at), added by V5 for the per-recipient daily cap's count, was dropped by V7, when the count moved to sms_recipient_sends

Notes

Shape of the DDL (V2__schema.sql names every constraint): CREATE TABLE notification_messages (id uuid PRIMARY KEY, appointment_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES appointments(id), kind varchar(16) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('CONFIRMATION','CANCELLATION')), channel varchar(8) NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('SMS','EMAIL')), recipient varchar(254) NOT NULL, subject varchar(160), body text NOT NULL, status varchar(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','SENT','FAILED')), attempts smallint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (attempts BETWEEN 0 AND 5), next_attempt_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_error varchar(500), failure_reason varchar(32) CHECK (failure_reason IN ('DELIVERY_FAILED','SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION','RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP','DISPATCH_ERROR')), provider_message_id varchar(64), correlation_id varchar(64) NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), sent_at timestamptz, CHECK ((channel = 'EMAIL') = (subject IS NOT NULL)), CHECK (channel <> 'SMS' OR char_length(body) <= 320), CHECK ((status = 'SENT') = (sent_at IS NOT NULL)), CHECK ((status = 'FAILED') = (failure_reason IS NOT NULL))); CREATE INDEX ix_notification_messages_due ON notification_messages (next_attempt_at) WHERE status = 'PENDING'. Rows are inserted by NotificationOutbox.enqueueConfirmation/enqueueCancellation with JdbcClient inside the booking/cancel transaction (SMS always; EMAIL only with the Tier 3 email channel, when customer_email is present), next_attempt_at = created_at = :now. Cancel (inside the cancel transaction, before the CANCELLATION insert, since V6): DELETE FROM notification_messages WHERE appointment_id = :appointmentId AND kind = 'CONFIRMATION', whatever the status (it waits at most one in-flight send for the row lock, so a confirmation is delivered before its cancellation or never); the cancel then deletes the appointment, and notification_messages_appointment_fkey, ON DELETE SET NULL since V6, leaves the CANCELLATION with no appointment, which notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only, CHECK (appointment_id IS NOT NULL OR kind = 'CANCELLATION'), allows for a cancellation alone. Dispatcher (NotificationDispatcher.runOnce), one transaction per row, up to 20 per tick: SELECT ... FROM notification_messages WHERE status = 'PENDING' AND next_attempt_at <= :now ORDER BY next_attempt_at, id LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED; send through MessageGateway; then success UPDATE ... SET status = 'SENT', attempts = :attempts, sent_at = :now, provider_message_id = :pid, last_error = NULL WHERE id = :id, or retry UPDATE ... SET attempts = :attempts, next_attempt_at = :next, last_error = :error WHERE id = :id (next = now + 2, 4, 8, 16 s after failures 1-4), or on the fifth failure, or on a refusal the gateway calls permanent, UPDATE ... SET status = 'FAILED', attempts = :attempts, failure_reason = 'DELIVERY_FAILED', last_error = :error WHERE id = :id; a CANCELLATION, which would be marked SENT or FAILED, is deleted instead with DELETE FROM notification_messages WHERE id = :id; commit. Since M6, before a CONFIRMATION is sent, the dispatcher asks booking whether its appointment is cancelled, by a plain read with no lock, and if so runs UPDATE ... SET status = 'FAILED', failure_reason = 'SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION' WHERE id = :id instead of sending. If the mark statement itself fails, a separate transaction runs UPDATE ... SET status = 'FAILED', failure_reason = 'DISPATCH_ERROR', last_error = 'MARK_FAILED' WHERE id = :id AND status = 'PENDING', or, for a CANCELLATION, DELETE FROM notification_messages WHERE id = :id AND status = 'PENDING'. Retry (RetryMessageHandler, M6), one transaction: SELECT ... FROM notification_messages WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE (no row -> 404 MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND); RetryPolicy allows only status FAILED with failure_reason DELIVERY_FAILED or DISPATCH_ERROR and, for a CONFIRMATION, an appointment neither cancelled nor started, which notification reads through booking's AppointmentDirectory rather than from appointments; then UPDATE notification_messages SET status = 'PENDING', attempts = 0, next_attempt_at = :now, last_error = NULL, failure_reason = NULL WHERE id = :id RETURNING the row, or 200 with the row unchanged. The runbook's SQL fallback, where the inbox is off, is a guarded UPDATE ... FROM appointments that applies the same rule: NOT (kind = 'CONFIRMATION' AND (the appointment is CANCELLED or has started)). Daily cap (where branchbook.sms.recipient-daily-cap is above 0): since V7 the count reads sms_recipient_sends, not this table; at or above the cap a CONFIRMATION is inserted FAILED with failure_reason RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP and attempts 0, and a CANCELLATION is not stored. Retention (where branchbook.retention.enabled is on): DELETE FROM notification_messages WHERE appointment_id IN (:ids), inside the purge's batch transaction. Not a JPA entity: every write above is an explicit statement. Owned by notification.
sms_recipient_sends 2 columns
ColumnTypeConstraints
recipient_fingerprintvarchar(64)NOT NULL, CHECK (recipient_fingerprint ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') (sms_recipient_sends_fingerprint_format); HMAC-SHA256 of the number's E.164 form under branchbook.sms.recipient-cap-key, in lower-case hex: never the number
written_attimestamptzNOT NULL; when the SMS was written, bound from the Clock

Indexes and constraints

  • No primary key: two SMS to one number can be written at the same instant, and each must count
  • ix_sms_recipient_sends_fingerprint (recipient_fingerprint, written_at) - the daily cap's count
  • ix_sms_recipient_sends_written (written_at) - the hourly purge

Notes

Added by V7: the per-recipient daily cap's ledger (BR-17). Where branchbook.sms.recipient-daily-cap is above 0, NotificationOutbox counts a number's rows with SELECT count(*) FROM sms_recipient_sends WHERE recipient_fingerprint = :fingerprint AND written_at > :since, :since = :now - 24 h, and, below the cap, adds one row in the booking or cancel transaction, so a rolled-back booking leaves none; a message the cap stopped adds none, and the demo block never reaches the table. A row outlives its message, so a confirmation a cancel deleted, or a cancellation deleted once final, still counts for its 24 hours. RecipientSendLedgerPurgeJob runs DELETE FROM sms_recipient_sends WHERE written_at <= :since every hour from a minute after start-up and logs cap_ledger.purged. The key must be at least 32 characters, or start-up stops; the hosted demo's is the branchbook/sms-cap-key secret in Secrets Manager, and a blank key gives a random one at each start, so every count starts again. No number, name, text, reference or foreign key, so no row leads back to a booking or a message. Written through JdbcClient by JdbcRecipientSendLedger behind notification's RecipientSendLedger port; owned by notification.

lookup_attempts 6 columns
ColumnTypeConstraints
referencevarchar(13)PRIMARY KEY (lookup_attempts_pkey), CHECK (reference ~ '^APT-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}$') (lookup_attempts_reference_format), the pattern of appointments_reference_format; no foreign key
opens_attimestamptzNOT NULL; the first admitted lookup of the window
closes_attimestamptzNOT NULL, CHECK (opens_at < closes_at) (lookup_attempts_window_order); opens_at plus branchbook.booking.lookup-window
held_untiltimestamptzNOT NULL, CHECK (held_until > opens_at) (lookup_attempts_lease_after_open); the lease of the newest lookup in flight, 30 seconds from its admission
failuressmallintNOT NULL; the wrong lookups in the window
in_flightsmallintNOT NULL, CHECK (failures >= 0 AND in_flight >= 0) (lookup_attempts_counts_not_negative); the admitted lookups whose digits are still being compared

Indexes and constraints

  • lookup_attempts_pkey (reference) - each count locks the reference's row by it
  • ix_lookup_attempts_closes (closes_at) - the hourly purge

Notes

Added by V8: the lookup limit's counts (BR-13), one row per reference with an open window, shared by every instance and kept across restarts. LookupAttemptLimiter reaches it through booking's LookupAttemptStore port, implemented by JdbcLookupAttemptStore: each count is its own short REQUIRES_NEW transaction that runs SELECT ... FROM lookup_attempts WHERE reference = :reference FOR UPDATE, then INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (reference) DO NOTHING for a first lookup, or an UPDATE or DELETE of the locked row, so lookups that arrive together, on any instance, are counted one at a time. Every instant is bound from the Clock. A closed window is ignored and replaced at the next lookup; LookupAttemptPurgeJob runs DELETE FROM lookup_attempts WHERE closes_at <= :now every hour from an hour after start-up and logs lookup_attempts.purged, so the table holds about an hour of wrongly looked-up references. There is deliberately no foreign key: a deleted reference must count exactly like one never issued. No name, number or appointment id. Owned by booking.

Migrations and seed data

  • V1__enable_btree_gist.sql: CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist, on its own so the schema migration only creates tables and constraints.
  • V2__schema.sql: the eight tables in dependency order (branches, branch_operating_hours, services, branch_services, public_holidays, appointments, idempotency_keys, notification_messages) with every named CHECK (including the timestamp-consistency CHECKs for cancelled_at, checked_in_at, completed_at, no_show_at, sent_at, the GSM-7 sms_name CHECKs, the tightened mobile pattern and the idempotency completion CHECK), UNIQUE, FK (incl. the composite appointments_branch_service_fkey), both EXCLUDE constraints, ix_appointments_customer_active, ix_appointments_slot_lookup, ix_idempotency_keys_expires, ix_notification_messages_due, ix_notification_messages_appointment, ix_notification_messages_created; a SQL comment at appointments_seat_no_overlap explaining '[)' adjacency and the three reasons for EXCLUDE rather than a (branch_id, service_id, seat_no, starts_at) unique index, and one at ix_appointments_customer_active explaining why it is not unique.
  • V3__reference_data.sql (no PII, fixed UUID literals so tests can reference them, plainly fictional street addresses): six branches - SAN 'Sandton Summit Mall' ('Shop 4, 1 Protea Lane', Sandton, Johannesburg, Gauteng, 2196), BEL 'Bellville Kingsway' ('12 Fynbos Street', Bellville, Cape Town, Western Cape, 7530), UMH 'Umhlanga Ridge' ('3 Strelitzia Walk', Umhlanga, Durban, KwaZulu-Natal, 4319), GQE 'Gqeberha Baywater' ('8 Aloe Crescent', Mill Park, Gqeberha, Eastern Cape, 6001), POL 'Polokwane Savannah' ('5 Marula Road', Polokwane Central, Polokwane, Limpopo, 0699), RUS 'Rustenburg Bojanala' ('9 Baobab Avenue', Rustenburg Central, Rustenburg, North West, 0299), sms_names 'Sandton Summit', 'Bellville Kingsway', 'Umhlanga Ridge', 'Gqeberha Baywater', 'Polokwane Savannah', 'Rustenburg Bojanala', all timezone Africa/Johannesburg, open_on_public_holidays false, active true; branch_operating_hours: every branch Mon-Fri (1-5) 08:00-17:00 and Sat (6) 08:00-13:00, except RUS Sat 08:00-12:00;
  • SAN and UMH add Sun (7) 09:00-13:00; no other Sunday rows (closed); six services with sort_order, sms_name and documents_needed (text[]): FICA 'Update personal details (FICA)' 15 min 'FICA update' ['Valid SA ID or passport with permit','Supporting document for the change, if any'];
  • CARD 'Card replacement or collection' 15 min 'Card replacement' ['Valid SA ID or passport','Your old card if you still have it'];
  • OPEN 'Open a personal account' 30 min 'Account opening' ['SA Smart ID or green ID book, or passport with a valid visa/permit','Cellphone registered in your name'];
  • CREDIT 'Credit or personal loan consultation' 30 min 'Credit consult' ['Valid SA ID','Latest payslip','Latest 3 months bank statements if your salary is not paid into Fynbos Bank'];
  • ESTATE 'Deceased estate assistance' 30 min 'Deceased estate' ['Certified copy of the death certificate','Certified copy of the deceased\'s ID','Your own ID','Letter of executorship, if issued'];
  • HOME 'Home loan consultation' 45 min 'Home loan consult' ['Valid SA ID','Latest 3 months payslips','Latest 3 months bank statements','Offer to purchase, if you have one']; branch_services capacities: POL 1 for all six;
  • SAN FICA 3, CREDIT 3, CARD 2, OPEN 2, ESTATE 2, HOME 2;
  • BEL, UMH, RUS 2 for all six;
  • GQE 2 for FICA, CARD, OPEN, CREDIT, ESTATE and no HOME row.
  • V4__public_holidays_2026_2027.sql: Schedule 1 dates only (source SCHEDULE_1) - 2026: 01-01 New Year's Day, 03-21 Human Rights Day (Sat, no substitute), 04-03 Good Friday, 04-06 Family Day, 04-27 Freedom Day, 05-01 Workers' Day, 06-16 Youth Day, 08-09 National Women's Day (Sun;
  • Mon 10th derived in code), 09-24 Heritage Day, 12-16 Day of Reconciliation, 12-25 Christmas Day, 12-26 Day of Goodwill (Sat, no substitute); 2027: 01-01, 03-21 Human Rights Day (Sun; 22nd derived), 03-26 Good Friday, 03-29 Family Day, 04-27, 05-01 (Sat), 06-16, 08-09, 09-24, 12-16, 12-25 (Sat), 12-26 Day of Goodwill (Sun; 27th derived); plus 2026-11-04 'Local Government Elections' source PROCLAMATION with a migration comment citing the 15 Sep 2026 Presidency statement (verified, and seeded with its citation).
  • Observed Mondays are never seeded;
  • PublicHolidayCalendar derives them with dated tests and reports coveredThrough() = 2027-12-31, which drives a WARN at start-up and every day at 06:00 SAST from 60 days before the booking window reaches it, and again once it has passed (from 2027-12-03); adding 2028 holidays is a new migration, V9 now that V5 to V8 are taken, not a code change.
  • V5__notification_messages_recipient_index.sql, added with real SMS: CREATE INDEX ix_notification_messages_recipient ON notification_messages (recipient, created_at), so the per-recipient daily cap reads one number's recent rows instead of every message written that day. V7 drops it.
  • V6__cancel_deletes_the_booking.sql: a cancel deletes the booking. notification_messages.appointment_id becomes nullable; notification_messages_appointment_fkey and idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey are re-created under the same names with ON DELETE SET NULL; notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only lets only a CANCELLATION have no appointment; and idempotency_keys_completed_consistent becomes CHECK (appointment_id IS NULL OR response_status IS NOT NULL), so a completed key outlives its cancelled appointment.
  • V7__sms_recipient_sends.sql: the daily cap's ledger, sms_recipient_sends, with sms_recipient_sends_fingerprint_format, ix_sms_recipient_sends_fingerprint and ix_sms_recipient_sends_written; it drops V5's index, which only the old count read.
  • V8__lookup_attempts.sql: the lookup limit's counts, lookup_attempts, with its primary key, four CHECKs and ix_lookup_attempts_closes.
  • The schema then holds ten tables and 61 named constraints: 9 primary keys, 3 unique, 41 checks, 6 foreign keys and 2 exclusion; SchemaConstraintIT.everyNamedConstraintHasACase fails if one is added without a case.
  • No migration contains appointments, idempotency keys, messages or any PII; demo bookings come from DemoDataLoader (M6), which runs only where branchbook.demo-data.enabled is on, which only compose.yaml sets, and books three fixtures through the real BookAppointmentHandler at the first free slot of the branch's next open day, as SlotCalendar finds it, only at Bellville Kingsway and Umhlanga Ridge (Polokwane Savannah stays empty for the two-tab race and scripts/demo-409.sh;
  • Sandton Summit is left empty too; scripts/smoke.sh writes nothing, and the README's example books there), with synthetic names, three distinct synthetic numbers outside the demo block (the third ends 0000, so its SMS always fails) and one correlation id, 'sys-' + UUID, so the slot grid and /messages show SENT and FAILED rows on first load; it runs on its own virtual thread once the application is ready, only when appointments is empty, books nothing where branchbook.booking.demo-numbers-only is on or where branchbook.sms.gateway is aws, so start-up never sends a real SMS, and logs instead of failing start-up on any error.
  • Retention: where branchbook.retention.enabled is on, as on the hosted demo, RetentionPurge deletes each appointment once its end has passed (plus branchbook.retention.after-end, zero by default), with its messages and idempotency keys, hourly from a minute after start-up: in batches of up to 500, each one transaction that runs SELECT id FROM appointments WHERE ends_at <= :cutoff ORDER BY ends_at, id LIMIT :limit FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, then deletes the messages, the keys and the appointments, in the order the foreign keys require. A booking for a future date is never touched. The RDS backups that could still hold a deleted booking expire after 7 days.
  • Reference vs transactional data: the five catalogue tables are immutable at runtime, so ITs reset only appointments, idempotency_keys, notification_messages, sms_recipient_sends and lookup_attempts (support/DatabaseReset: TRUNCATE notification_messages, idempotency_keys, appointments, sms_recipient_sends, lookup_attempts
  • without CASCADE, before and after each test, also clearing ScriptedReferenceGenerator; ResetClockExtension puts MutableClock back to a fixed Monday instant before every IT).
  • Flyway's flyway_schema_history is the migration audit; no ddl-auto=update anywhere.

DesignBuilt

API

Status. Built: the branch, availability, booking and lookup endpoints, the committed OpenAPI contract with Swagger UI, the problem model with 21 codes, correlation ids, health probes and SPA forwarding; from M5, GET /api/v1/messages, the simulated inbox, behind branchbook.inbox.enabled, on under make up and make dev-api and off on the hosted demo; and in M6, POST /api/v1/appointments/{reference}/cancel, POST /api/v1/messages/{id}/retry, which exists only with the inbox, and /actuator/prometheus, which the hosted demo does not expose; and since M6, a cancel that deletes the booking (V6) and lookup counts shared in PostgreSQL (V8).

Style
Resource-oriented REST over JSON on one origin (the Spring Boot jar serves the SPA, so there is no CORS). Paths use plural nouns; the only verbs are the state transition /cancel and the ops action /retry. Request and response records are Java records that springdoc maps into docs/openapi.yaml (OpenAPI 3.1, set explicitly with springdoc.api-docs.version). OpenApiContractIT fails CI on drift, and openapi-typescript generates web/src/api/schema.d.ts. Companion headers: X-Correlation-Id on every call (accepted if <= 64 chars of [A-Za-z0-9-], otherwise generated; always echoed); Idempotency-Key on POST /appointments only; X-Mobile-Last4 on lookup and cancel. Instants are ISO-8601 with the branch offset (2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00, never Z). They are produced by one helper, shared.time.TimeFormat.isoOffset(instant, zone) = instant.atZone(zone).toOffsetDateTime() formatted with ISO_OFFSET_DATE_TIME, and carried in response records as String with @Schema(format = "date-time"). No Jackson time-zone feature is relied on, so the output is identical under Jackson 2 and Jackson 3, and AvailabilityApiIT.rendersStartsAtWithJohannesburgOffset is the guard. The request startsAt is a String validated by @IsoOffsetDateTime and parsed with OffsetDateTime.parse(ISO_OFFSET_DATE_TIME). Strict JSON: FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES is enabled explicitly on the JSON mapper (Boot disables it by default), so unknown properties return 400 VALIDATION_FAILED (pinned by AppointmentApiIT.postWithUnknownJsonFieldReturns400ValidationFailed). There is no in-app body-size limit in v1: the request-body size filter (413 PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE) is cut in the delivery plan and left to the gateway/WAF. Cache-Control: no-store on /api/**; caching is the SPA's job (TanStack Query staleTime).
Base path
/api/v1
Versioning
Major version in the path (/api/v1). Within v1 only additive, backward-compatible changes are allowed: new optional fields, and new codes documented in docs/errors.md (this revision adds MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND, failureReason, retryable, nextAttemptAt, bookableFrom and bookableTo, all before first release, and DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED). Also before first release, from V6, a repeat cancel answers 404 rather than a 200 no-op, and IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED also refuses the key of a booking since cancelled. A breaking change would ship as /api/v2 beside v1; none is planned. The committed docs/openapi.yaml is the contract. Any change to it is a reviewed diff in the same commit as the server change and the regenerated schema.d.ts, with CI drift checks on both sides. Actuator, /v3/api-docs and /swagger-ui.html are unversioned infrastructure paths.
Error model
RFC 9457 application/problem+json from shared.problem.ProblemDetailHandler, with ErrorDispatchController for failures outside Spring MVC. Fields: type 'urn:branchbook:problem:<CODE>'; title (short, plain); status; detail (plain language, never echoes input, no PII); instance (urn:branchbook:request:<correlationId>, never the path); code (stable, catalogued in docs/errors.md); correlationId. Extensions: errors[{field, code, message}] for 400 and 422 field problems, and alternatives[{startsAt, endsAt}] for 409 SLOT_FULL (up to 3). No problem body ever carries an appointment reference, a booked time, a name or any mobile digits; existingReference no longer exists. Status split: - 400 VALIDATION_FAILED for shape problems, with errors[]: blank, length, pattern, malformed JSON, unknown field, startsAt without an offset (code OFFSET_REQUIRED), and a malformed or missing X-Mobile-Last4 (not counted by the limiter, because a shape check reveals nothing). - 400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED. - 422 <DOMAIN_CODE> when the shape is fine but a domain rule rejects it. MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED carries errors[{field:'customer.mobile'}]; SLOT_* codes carry errors[{field:'startsAt'}]. - 404, 409, 429 and 503 as listed per endpoint. Precedence is fixed, so one request always yields one code: - 422: DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW > SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY > SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS > SLOT_OFF_GRID > SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME (pinned by SlotCalendarTest.validatePrecedence). - 409, evaluated under the per-mobile lock: DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT > CUSTOMER_OVERLAP > SLOT_FULL. The two customer-conflict 409s are deliberately generic. They still confirm that some booking exists for a number the caller typed; this is accepted for the demo and closed in production by verified identity (ADR-0004). 503 TRY_AGAIN carries Retry-After. It is returned for: - lock_timeout 55P03 and statement_timeout 57014: Retry-After 2. - 40P01 deadlock and 40001 serialization failure, after one automatic re-run of the transaction: Retry-After 2. - Pool timeout (CannotGetJdbcConnectionException or CannotCreateTransactionException after HikariCP's 3 s): Retry-After 5. 500 INTERNAL_ERROR carries only correlationId; stack traces are never serialised (server.error.include-stacktrace=never). The only problems without a code are 404, 405, 406 and 415, for unknown routes, methods, Accept headers the API cannot satisfy and media types, which includes /api/v1/messages while branchbook.inbox.enabled is false, the default. They carry no type either, which RFC 9457 reads as about:blank, but still carry instance and correlationId. Example 409: {"type":"urn:branchbook:problem:SLOT_FULL","title":"That time was just taken","status":409,"detail":"All seats for this time were booked while you were completing your details. Choose one of the alternatives.","instance":"urn:branchbook:request:<correlationId>","code":"SLOT_FULL","correlationId":"3f6d2c0e-7a1b-4d7e-9f0c-2b1a9e8d7c6b","alternatives":[{"startsAt":"2026-10-27T09:45:00+02:00","endsAt":"2026-10-27T10:00:00+02:00"},{"startsAt":"2026-10-27T10:00:00+02:00","endsAt":"2026-10-27T10:15:00+02:00"}]}. Example 409 duplicate: {"type":"urn:branchbook:problem:DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT","title":"You already have this appointment","status":409,"detail":"You already have an upcoming appointment for this service. Use the reference in your SMS to manage it.","instance":"urn:branchbook:request:<correlationId>","code":"DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT","correlationId":"..."}. Example 422: {"type":"urn:branchbook:problem:MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED","title":"We need a cellphone number","status":422,"detail":"We need a cellphone number to SMS your confirmation.","instance":"urn:branchbook:request:<correlationId>","code":"MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED","correlationId":"...","errors":[{"field":"customer.mobile","code":"MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED","message":"Enter a South African cellphone number, for example 082 555 0101"}]}. Example 400: {"type":"urn:branchbook:problem:VALIDATION_FAILED","title":"Some details need attention","status":400,"detail":"One or more fields are missing or invalid.","instance":"urn:branchbook:request:<correlationId>","code":"VALIDATION_FAILED","correlationId":"...","errors":[{"field":"customer.firstName","code":"NOT_BLANK","message":"is required"},{"field":"startsAt","code":"OFFSET_REQUIRED","message":"must be an ISO-8601 date and time with an offset"}]}. The catalogue has 21 codes: the 19 in the spine plus MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND and DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED (there is no PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE: the request-body size filter is cut, and body limits belong to the gateway/WAF). The SPA maps code to copy in web/src/api/problemCopy.ts and never string-matches detail.
Access model
No customer authentication (ADR-0004). Spring Security is stateless, with permitAll on every request and the reason written in SecurityConfig. CSRF is off because there are no cookies or sessions. Headers: CSP default-src 'self' (font self-hosted; /swagger-ui/** alone also allows inline styles), X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, frame-ancestors 'none', Referrer-Policy no-referrer, Cache-Control no-store on /api. Access to an appointment requires possession of the reference (31^8 space, SecureRandom) plus knowledge of the last 4 digits of the booking mobile, sent as header X-Mobile-Last4 on GET /appointments/{reference} and POST .../cancel. Any mismatch, including an unknown well-formed reference and a cancelled one, whose appointment the cancel deleted, returns 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND with an identical body, never 403. The digits are compared in constant time (MessageDigest.isEqual). LookupAttemptLimiter (per reference, counted from V8 in the PostgreSQL table lookup_attempts, so every instance shares the count and a restart keeps it; each count is a short transaction of its own that locks the reference's row, a lookup in flight holds its place for 30 seconds at most, and an hourly LookupAttemptPurgeJob deletes closed windows) allows 5 failures in a fixed window that opens with the first and lasts an hour, then returns 429 LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED with Retry-After for every attempt on that reference, correct digits included. Each lookup is counted as in flight before the database is read, then kept as a failure (wrong digits) or released (right digits, or a read that failed), so lookups that arrive together cannot all pass; one refused only because failures and lookups in flight together reach 5 gets Retry-After: 1. A block that let correct digits through would make brute force free. Malformed references are rejected before any query and never touch the limiter. Accepted trade-off: someone who already holds a reference can block lookups for it for an hour. Production closes this with OIDC sessions and gateway/WAF limits. Masking: there are two renderings of the same field, recipientMasked / mobileMasked. - Digit-bearing ('082 *** 0101', email 'n***a@example.com'): only in responses to a caller who has just proved the number, i.e. the POST 201 that sent it, and GET or cancel after the last-4 check. - Digit-free ('082 *** ****', email 'n***@example.com'): everywhere else, i.e. GET /api/v1/messages, the retry response, logs, MDC, metrics and health. The digits never appear in URLs, logs, problem bodies or the inbox. Log lines carry messageId, kind and channel, never a recipient, and name appointments by id, never by reference. /api/v1/messages exists only while branchbook.inbox.enabled is true: compose.yaml, make dev-api and the tests switch it on, and off, the default and the hosted demo's setting, it is a plain 404. /retry, built in M6, exists only with it. Switched on, the inbox is an unauthenticated demo operator view that lists every reference and message body; recipients are digit-free, compose binds ports to 127.0.0.1, and the hosted demo keeps it off: with no sign-in and public demo numbers, its references would let anyone open any booking. The hosted demo also sets branchbook.inbox.redact-references, so were the inbox switched on there, it and its retry would show every reference as APT-****-****. Actuator exposes health, info and prometheus (M6); the hosted demo's task narrows it to health and info, and its WAF blocks /actuator. There are no staff or admin endpoints. Threat model for an unauthenticated endpoint that sends SMS: - SMS pumping or toll fraud, harassment of third parties, and slot squatting with invented valid numbers. - In-app controls: Idempotency-Key, the per-mobile rules (one upcoming appointment per service, no overlaps), capacity bounds per slot, and the per-recipient daily cap, branchbook.sms.recipient-daily-cap: off by default, 5 SMS per number per rolling 24 h on the hosted demo, never for the demo block, counted in the ledger sms_recipient_sends by a keyed fingerprint of the number, so the messages a cancel deleted still count (a further confirmation is written FAILED / RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP and a further cancellation is reported so but never stored; neither is sent, and the booking or cancel still stands). - On the hosted demo, which texts real SMS: a protect configuration that allows SMS to South Africa only, a max price of US$0.20 a message, AWS's monthly SMS spend limit, whose refusal fails each message at once, and WAF's limit of 10 POSTs per IP in 5 minutes under /api/v1/appointments. - Production adds (README): OTP verification of the mobile before CONFIRMED (or booking only inside the authenticated banking app), CAPTCHA on POST, per-IP and per-number-prefix gateway limits, premium-prefix blocking, provider fraud and geo controls, SMS spend alerts, and slot-hold limits per verified identity. Production path for identity (README): OIDC against the bank's IdP, with appointments bound to a customer id.

Endpoints

GET /api/v1/branchesActive branches for the BranchStep.

Active branches for the BranchStep.

Request

No body. Optional header X-Correlation-Id.

Response

200 {"branches":[{"code":"SAN","name":"Sandton Summit Mall","suburb":"Sandton","city":"Johannesburg","province":"Gauteng","opensSunday":true},{"code":"POL","name":"Polokwane Savannah","suburb":"Polokwane Central","city":"Polokwane","province":"Limpopo","opensSunday":false}]}

Status codes

  • 200 OK: list of active branches ordered by name (six seeded; inactive branches omitted)
  • 500 INTERNAL_ERROR: unexpected failure, correlationId only
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN: database unreachable (HikariCP connectionTimeout 3 s), Retry-After: 5

Notes

Bounded (<= 6 rows), no pagination by design. HTTP Cache-Control: no-store; the SPA caches with staleTime 5 min. No validation inputs. Served from catalogue read-only projections.

GET /api/v1/branches/{code}Branch detail for ServiceStep and DateStrip: address, timezone, hours, services offered,…

Branch detail for ServiceStep and DateStrip: address, timezone, hours, services offered, the server-computed bookable window, and the closed dates inside it.

Request

Path code matching ^[A-Z]{2,8}$ after upper-casing. No body.

Response

200 {"code":"SAN","name":"Sandton Summit Mall","smsName":"Sandton Summit","address":{"line1":"Shop 4, 1 Protea Lane","suburb":"Sandton","city":"Johannesburg","province":"Gauteng","postalCode":"2196"},"timezone":"Africa/Johannesburg","bookableFrom":"2026-10-19","bookableTo":"2026-11-17","operatingHours":[{"weekday":1,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"17:00"},{"weekday":2,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"17:00"},{"weekday":3,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"17:00"},{"weekday":4,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"17:00"},{"weekday":5,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"17:00"},{"weekday":6,"opensAt":"08:00","closesAt":"13:00"},{"weekday":7,"opensAt":"09:00","closesAt":"13:00"}],"services":[{"code":"FICA","name":"Update personal details (FICA)","durationMinutes":15,"documentsNeeded":["Valid SA ID or passport with permit","Supporting document for the change, if any"]}],"closedDates":[{"date":"2026-11-04","reason":"PUBLIC_HOLIDAY","name":"Local Government Elections"}]}. SAN trades every day, so only holidays appear (the 4 Nov PROCLAMATION row is seeded in V4). GET /branches/POL on the same day also lists {"date":"2026-10-25","reason":"CLOSED_DAY","name":null} and the Sundays 2026-11-01, 11-08 and 11-15.

Status codes

  • 200 OK
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: code not matching ^[A-Z]{2,8}$
  • 404 BRANCH_NOT_FOUND: unknown or inactive code
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN: database unavailable

Notes

weekday is ISO 1-7 (java DayOfWeek.getValue()). A weekday without a row is closed and appears in closedDates as CLOSED_DAY. bookableFrom = today and bookableTo = today + 29 days are computed in branch.timezone from the Clock bean. The window is half-open [today, today + P30D), i.e. 30 bookable dates, so the DateStrip renders exactly this range and never works the window out from the device's date (DateTimeStep.test.tsx 'pages only within the booking window'). closedDates covers that same range using PublicHolidayCalendar, including s2(1) derived Mondays. Services are ordered by sort_order, and only this branch's branch_services rows appear. The SPA caches the response for 5 min; the DateStrip needs no further call until a date is chosen.

GET /api/v1/branches/{code}/availability?service=FICA&date=2026-10-27One day's bookable slots for one service at one branch (SlotGrid).

One day's bookable slots for one service at one branch (SlotGrid).

Request

Path code ^[A-Za-z]{2,8}$ and query service ^[A-Za-z]{2,12}$ (required), both upper-cased; date yyyy-MM-dd (required). No body.

Response

200 {"branchCode":"SAN","serviceCode":"FICA","date":"2026-10-27","timezone":"Africa/Johannesburg","generatedAt":"2026-10-19T10:05:12+02:00","closedReason":null,"holidayName":null,"slots":[{"startsAt":"2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00","endsAt":"2026-10-27T09:45:00+02:00","seatsAvailable":2},{"startsAt":"2026-10-27T09:45:00+02:00","endsAt":"2026-10-27T10:00:00+02:00","seatsAvailable":0}]}  Closed day (POL is closed on Sundays): GET /api/v1/branches/POL/availability?service=FICA&date=2026-10-25 -> 200 {"branchCode":"POL","serviceCode":"FICA","date":"2026-10-25","timezone":"Africa/Johannesburg","generatedAt":"...","closedReason":"CLOSED_DAY","holidayName":null,"slots":[]}

Status codes

  • 200 OK (closed day returns empty slots plus closedReason CLOSED_DAY | PUBLIC_HOLIDAY with holidayName)
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: missing/invalid code, service or date
  • 404 BRANCH_NOT_FOUND
  • 422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH: no branch_services row, or no active service with that code
  • 422 DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW: date outside [bookableFrom, bookableTo] (branch zone)
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN

Notes

SlotCalendar is pure: SlotCalendar(branch calendar, service offer, now, PT2H, P30D), and slotsOn(date, booked) takes the day's booked seats. Slots start at opens_at + k*duration and must end by closes_at; examples: last 30-minute weekday start 16:30, last 45-minute Saturday start 11:45, last 45-minute Rustenburg Saturday start 11:00. Slots inside the lead time or in the past are omitted. Full slots are returned with seatsAvailable 0 so the grid can show 'Full'. Booked seats come from BookedIntervalsPort: the active (CONFIRMED or CHECKED_IN) appointments of that service at that branch that overlap the day, read with a period the caller binds, never now(). The read starts 120 minutes early, the longest appointment the schema allows, so it uses one day of the slot-lookup index instead of every past appointment. generatedAt lets the UI show staleness; the SPA refetches on focus and when 60 s stale. AvailabilityApiIT.rendersStartsAtWithJohannesburgOffset pins '+02:00'. One day per call keeps the response bounded (<= 36 slots: 08:00-17:00 at 15 minutes).

POST /api/v1/appointmentsBook an appointment. This is the one write transaction (BookAppointmentHandler).

Book an appointment. This is the one write transaction (BookAppointmentHandler).

Request

Headers: Idempotency-Key: 8c4f1a2e-1b3d-4e5f-9a6b-7c8d9e0f1a2b (UUID, required), Content-Type: application/json, X-Correlation-Id optional. Body: {"branchCode":"SAN","serviceCode":"FICA","startsAt":"2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00","customer":{"firstName":"Nomvula","lastName":"Dlamini","mobile":"082 555 0101","email":"nomvula@example.com"}}. email is optional and exists only if the Tier 3 email channel ships. endsAt is never accepted (unknown field gives 400). Names are NFC-normalised and curly apostrophes (U+2019, U+2018, U+02BC) mapped to ASCII before validation. The email channel is Tier 3 and cut by default; unless it ships, customer.email is absent from the record, the spec and the form.

Response

201 Created, Location: /api/v1/appointments/APT-7K3M-9Q2X. Body AppointmentResponse: {"reference":"APT-7K3M-9Q2X","status":"CONFIRMED","branch":{"code":"SAN","name":"Sandton Summit Mall","address":"Shop 4, 1 Protea Lane, Sandton, Johannesburg, 2196"},"service":{"code":"FICA","name":"Update personal details (FICA)","durationMinutes":15,"documentsNeeded":["Valid SA ID or passport with permit"]},"startsAt":"2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00","endsAt":"2026-10-27T09:45:00+02:00","customer":{"firstName":"Nomvula","lastName":"Dlamini","mobileMasked":"082 *** 0101"},"canCancel":true,"cancelledAt":null,"notifications":[{"id":"c1d2...","channel":"SMS","kind":"CONFIRMATION","status":"PENDING","attempts":0,"sentAt":null,"failureReason":null,"recipientMasked":"082 *** 0101","body":"Appt confirmed: FICA update, Sandton Summit, Tue 27 Oct 09:30 SAST. Ref APT-7K3M-9Q2X. Bring ID + docs. Manage: http://localhost:8080/manage"},{"id":"e3f4...","channel":"EMAIL","kind":"CONFIRMATION","status":"PENDING","attempts":0,"sentAt":null,"failureReason":null,"recipientMasked":"n***a@example.com","body":"Hi Nomvula,\n\nYour appointment is confirmed..."}]}. The EMAIL item appears only if the Tier 3 email channel ships. Replay: 201, the same Location, header Idempotent-Replayed: true, and the body re-rendered from the current state of the same appointment. Once that appointment is cancelled, and so deleted, the same key is 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED and never books again.

Status codes

  • 201 Created: booked (or idempotent replay with Idempotent-Replayed: true)
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: field errors (names blank, > 60 or invalid characters; email shape or > 254; startsAt missing or without an offset (OFFSET_REQUIRED); unknown JSON field)
  • 400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED: header missing or not a UUID
  • No 413: the request-body size filter is cut in v1 (body limits are a gateway/WAF concern)
  • 404 BRANCH_NOT_FOUND: unknown or inactive branch
