  • 422 MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED: not an SA mobile (landline 01-05, VoIP 087, 080/086)
  • 422 DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED: only where branchbook.booking.demo-numbers-only is on (off by default, and off on the hosted demo since it sends real SMS): a South African mobile outside 082 555 0000 to 0199; nothing is written
  • 422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH
  • 422 DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW | SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY | SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS | SLOT_OFF_GRID | SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME (in that precedence): SlotCalendar.validate, errors[{field:'startsAt'}]
  • 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED: same live key, different request_hash; or the same hash after the booking the key made was cancelled, with a fixed detail saying so, and nothing booked
  • 409 DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT: this mobile already holds an upcoming active appointment (ends_at > now) for this service at any branch; no reference or time in the body
  • 409 CUSTOMER_OVERLAP: this mobile holds an active appointment overlapping [startsAt, endsAt) at any branch (pre-check; appointments_customer_no_overlap is the backstop); no reference or time in the body
  • 409 SLOT_FULL: no free seat; alternatives[] (<= 3 later free slots the same day, computed after rollback outside the lock)
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN: lock_timeout or statement_timeout (Retry-After: 2); deadlock or serialization failure after one automatic re-run (Retry-After: 2); pool timeout (Retry-After: 5)

Notes

Transaction order is fixed (the ten numbered steps of BookAppointmentHandler.handle). One TransactionTemplate at READ COMMITTED, with :now bound from the Clock in every statement: 1. Idempotency INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE ... WHERE the stored key has expired, RETURNING key. 2. Load the active branch and its branch_services row. 3. SlotCalendar.validate(startsAt, clock); compute ends_at. 4. pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(:mobileE164, 0)). 5. The DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT pre-check, then the CUSTOMER_OVERLAP pre-check. 6. SELECT branch_services ... FOR UPDATE. 7. Lowest free seat, or 409 SLOT_FULL. 8. INSERT the appointment via EntityManager.persist and flush immediately. 9. ConfirmationOutbox.enqueueConfirmation, booking's port, which NotificationOutbox implements with one JdbcClient INSERT (Propagation.MANDATORY). 10. UPDATE idempotency_keys SET appointment_id, response_status = 201. Then commit. Lock order is idempotency PK, then mobile, then branch_services. It is the same in every transaction, so these locks cannot deadlock. Same-mobile requests serialise before the seat lock, so the exclusion-constraint deadlock between two branches is impossible. Backstop translation: ConstraintViolationTranslator reads ServerErrorMessage.getConstraint() and getSQLState() and never parses message text. - appointments_seat_no_overlap (23P01): SLOT_FULL. - appointments_customer_no_overlap (23P01): CUSTOMER_OVERLAP. - appointments_reference_key (23505): re-run the transaction once with a fresh reference. - 40P01 / 40001: re-run once, then 503. Mobile numbers are accepted in six formats, stored as E.164 and masked as described. Email is lower-cased. Metric (built in M6, counted by BookingOutcomeMeter once the response is written) appointments_booked_total{outcome=confirmed|replayed|slot_full|duplicate|customer_overlap|validation|try_again}; 500s are counted by http_server_requests_seconds. One booking.confirmed INFO line per booking, with appointmentId, branch, service and seat after the request's correlationId; never the reference. Names, digits and references never appear (LogRedactionIT). pgjdbc logServerErrorDetail=false and Hibernate's SQL error log (org.hibernate.orm.jdbc.error) OFF keep constraint DETAIL, which contains the mobile, out of the logs. No per-IP limit in-app (a gateway concern); the per-recipient SMS cap lives in NotificationOutbox, counted in the sms_recipient_sends ledger (V7), off by default and 5 a day on the hosted demo. Tests: AppointmentApiIT.postReturns201WithLocationReferenceAndPendingNotifications, noProblemBodyEverContainsAnAppointmentReference, conflict409BodiesNeverContainReferenceOrStartsAt; BookAppointmentIT.sameMobileSameServiceSecondBookingReturns409WithoutRevealingReference, pastConfirmedAppointmentDoesNotBlockSameServiceBooking; ConcurrentBookingIT.sameMobileTwoBranchesOverlappingConcurrentlyGivesOne201AndOne409CustomerOverlap.

GET /api/v1/appointments/{reference}View one appointment: confirmation page, manage page, and delivery-status polling.

View one appointment: confirmation page, manage page, and delivery-status polling.

Request

Path reference: trimmed, upper-cased, hyphens optional (apt7k3m9q2x is accepted). Header X-Mobile-Last4: 0101 (exactly 4 digits). No body.

Response

200 AppointmentResponse (same shape as POST) with the current delivery state, e.g. "notifications":[{"id":"c1d2...","channel":"SMS","kind":"CONFIRMATION","status":"SENT","attempts":1,"sentAt":"2026-10-19T10:05:15+02:00","failureReason":null,"recipientMasked":"082 *** 0101","body":"Appt confirmed: ..."}]

Status codes

  • 200 OK
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: X-Mobile-Last4 missing or not ^\d{4}$ (shape only; not counted as a failed attempt)
  • 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND: unknown well-formed reference, including one a cancel deleted, or wrong digits, identical body, digits compared in constant time; counts one failure for that reference. A malformed reference gets the same 404 with no query and no count.
  • 429 LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED: once 5 failures for the reference fall within the window, every attempt (including correct digits) until it resets, with Retry-After in seconds until then; Retry-After: 1 when refused only because failures and lookups still being checked together reach 5
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN

Notes

canCancel = status == CONFIRMED && clock.now() < startsAt. ConfirmationPage polls this endpoint every 2 s while any notification is PENDING. Polling stops at SENT or FAILED, with no time cap; a 0000 recipient reaches FAILED in about 35 s. failureReason drives the FAILED copy: DELIVERY_FAILED, SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION, RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP (where the daily cap is on) or DISPATCH_ERROR. The limiter counts each lookup of a well-formed reference as in flight before the read and keeps only mismatches as failures; a success resets nothing (fixed window per reference). Digits are compared with MessageDigest.isEqual against the last 4 of customer_mobile. They are never logged. Cache-Control: no-store.

POST /api/v1/appointments/{reference}/cancelBuilt in M6. CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED by the customer before the start time, and the booking deleted at once (V6)…

Built in M6. CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED by the customer before the start time, and the booking deleted at once (V6). In the same transaction it deletes every confirmation of the appointment, whatever its status, queues the cancellation SMS and deletes the appointment, which frees the seat. Only the cancellation SMS, until it is final, and the idempotency key, until its 24 hours end, are kept, with no appointment.

Request

Header X-Mobile-Last4: 0101. Empty body (or {}). X-Correlation-Id optional.

Response

200 AppointmentResponse, built in the transaction from the appointment just before it is deleted, with "status":"CANCELLED","canCancel":false,"cancelledAt":"2026-10-19T10:20:03+02:00". notifications[] holds only the cancellation SMS: {"channel":"SMS","kind":"CANCELLATION","status":"PENDING","attempts":0,"sentAt":null,"failureReason":null,"recipientMasked":"082 *** 0101","body":"Appt cancelled: FICA update, Sandton Summit, Tue 27 Oct 09:30 SAST. Ref APT-7K3M-9Q2X. Rebook: http://localhost:8080/book"}. No confirmation is listed: the cancel deleted them. Where the daily cap stops the cancellation SMS, it shows status FAILED with failureReason RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP and is never stored.

Status codes

  • 200 OK: cancelled and deleted; the body is the appointment as the cancel left it, with only its cancellation SMS
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: header shape
  • 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND: unknown reference or wrong digits (counts a failure, with lookups, towards the same five); also, counted the same way, an appointment already cancelled and so deleted, by an earlier cancel or by a second cancel at the same moment, and one the retention purge deleted once it was over; never a 500
  • 409 TOO_LATE_TO_CANCEL: clock.now() >= startsAt (status unchanged)
  • 429 LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN: lock_timeout, such as on a confirmation still being sent, statement_timeout or pool timeout; nothing is changed

Notes

CancelAppointmentHandler runs steps 2 to 9 in one READ COMMITTED transaction: 1. Count the try with the limiter, in a short transaction of the limiter's own. 2. Load the appointment by reference through JPA (no pessimistic lock). 3. Constant-time digit check. 4. Appointment.cancel(now) in memory, guarded by AppointmentStatus.canTransitionTo(CANCELLED) and the time guard. 5. Lock the appointment's row with its version checked (JpaAppointmentRepository.lockForDeletion), so a cancel or purge that got there first is found before anything is written. 6. NotificationOutbox.enqueueCancellation: delete every CONFIRMATION of the appointment, whatever its status, waiting for one the dispatcher is sending, then insert the PENDING CANCELLATION rows (SMS always; EMAIL only with the Tier 3 email channel; where the number has reached the daily cap, reported FAILED / RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP and never stored). 7. Render the response from the appointment in memory and that one SMS. 8. Delete the appointment with its version checked; notification_messages_appointment_fkey and idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey, ON DELETE SET NULL from V6, keep the cancellation SMS and the idempotency key with no appointment. 9. Commit. 10. Settle the count, in a transaction of the limiter's own: a 404 is a failure, anything else releases the try. From the commit the reference is unknown: a lookup or a repeat cancel is 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND and counts as a wrong try (CancelAppointmentIT.lookupAndRepeatCancelAfterACancelReturn404AndAreCounted). Of two concurrent cancels (two tabs), the second waits for the first's lock, finds the row gone and answers that counted 404 (CancelAppointmentIT.twoConcurrentCancelsGiveOne200AndOne404ThatIsCounted); so does a cancel whose appointment the retention purge deleted after it was loaded. There is no re-read. The seat is freed immediately because the appointment row is deleted (CancelAppointmentIT.cancelFreesSeatForNewBooking). CHECKED_IN, COMPLETED and NO_SHOW are unreachable via the API. There is no reschedule endpoint: the customer cancels, then rebooks with branch and service pre-filled.

GET /api/v1/messages?limit=50Simulated inbox: the newest notification_messages across all appointments, only while switched on…

Simulated inbox: the newest notification_messages across all appointments, only while branchbook.inbox.enabled is true. It is an unauthenticated demo operator view.

Request

Query limit integer 1-200, default 50. No body.

Response

200 {"messages":[{"id":"c1d2...","reference":"APT-7K3M-9Q2X","kind":"CONFIRMATION","channel":"SMS","recipientMasked":"082 *** ****","subject":null,"body":"Appt confirmed: ...","status":"FAILED","failureReason":"DELIVERY_FAILED","retryable":true,"attempts":5,"nextAttemptAt":null,"lastError":"SIMULATED_FAILURE","createdAt":"2026-10-19T10:05:12+02:00","sentAt":null},{"id":"e3f4...","reference":"APT-7K3M-9Q2X","kind":"CONFIRMATION","channel":"EMAIL","recipientMasked":"n***@example.com","subject":"Appointment confirmed - APT-7K3M-9Q2X | FICA update, Tuesday 27 October 2026 at 09:30","body":"Hi Nomvula, ...","status":"SENT","failureReason":null,"retryable":false,"attempts":1,"nextAttemptAt":null,"lastError":null,"createdAt":"...","sentAt":"..."}]}

Status codes

  • 200 OK: ordered by createdAt desc, then id
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: limit outside 1-200 or not an integer
  • 404 (Spring default problem, no code): controller absent while branchbook.inbox.enabled is false, the default (InboxSwitchedOffIT.inboxIsAPlain404WhenSwitchedOff)
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN

Notes

InboxController, @ConditionalOnProperty(branchbook.inbox.enabled); the setting is the only gate. Recipients are always digit-free (InboxApiIT.listsTheNewestMessagesWithTheirReferencesAndRedactedRecipients), and lastError never contains recipient data. The full body is shown because it is the demo artefact; the page therefore exposes every reference and, for email, the first name. That is why it is banner-labelled, bound to 127.0.0.1 and absent unless a deployment switches it on (the hosted demo does not) (InboxSwitchedOffIT starts a second context with it off and gets a plain 404). retryable comes from RetryPolicy, the rule the retry itself applies: status FAILED and failureReason IN (DELIVERY_FAILED, DISPATCH_ERROR), and for a CONFIRMATION, an appointment neither cancelled nor started; the runbook's replay SQL refuses the same. Where branchbook.inbox.redact-references is on, every reference shows as APT-****-****, in the reference field, the body, the subject and the last error, while message ids stay real, so a retry still works (InboxReferencesHiddenIT). Instants are rendered in the appointment's branch timezone, or in Africa/Johannesburg for a cancellation whose booking the cancel deleted, which shows APT-****-**** as its reference and leaves the list once it is sent or has failed for good. There is no cursor pagination (a bounded demo). InboxPage polls every 2 s while any row is PENDING, and otherwise fetches again when the page is opened. The banner text 'Demo operator view. It lists every booking's messages, recipients redacted, and is switched off in production. Here every message is simulated, as on any local run.' is UI copy.

POST /api/v1/messages/{id}/retryReplay a FAILED message: the runbook's one action, as a button. Built in M6; only while the inbox…

Replay a FAILED message: the runbook's one action, as a button. Built in M6; only while the inbox is switched on (branchbook.inbox.enabled), and elsewhere the runbook's guarded SQL replays a message.

Request

Path id: UUID. No body.

Response

200 MessageResponse (same shape as a /messages item): {"id":"c1d2...","reference":"APT-7K3M-9Q2X","kind":"CONFIRMATION","channel":"SMS","recipientMasked":"082 *** ****","subject":null,"body":"Appt confirmed: ...","status":"PENDING","failureReason":null,"retryable":false,"attempts":0,"nextAttemptAt":"2026-10-19T10:31:00+02:00","lastError":null,"createdAt":"...","sentAt":null}

Status codes

  • 200 OK: FAILED and retryable -> PENDING (attempts 0, next_attempt_at :now, last_error and failure_reason cleared)
  • 200 OK no-op: PENDING, SENT or non-retryable FAILED rows (SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION, RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP), and a FAILED confirmation of an appointment that is cancelled or has started, are returned unchanged with retryable=false (RetryMessageApiIT.retryOfSupersededConfirmationIsNoOp, retryOfAFailedConfirmationOfACancelledAppointmentIsANoOp)
  • 400 VALIDATION_FAILED: id not a UUID
  • 404 MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND: unknown id, including a cancellation deleted once it was sent or failed for good (RetryMessageApiIT.retryOfUnknownIdReturns404MessageNotFound, retryOfACancellationWithNoAppointmentIsANoOpUntilItIsSentThen404)
  • 404 (Spring default problem, no code): while branchbook.inbox.enabled is false, the default, where the route is absent
  • 503 TRY_AGAIN: the message stayed locked for 2 seconds, or the database is unavailable

Notes

RetryMessageHandler, one transaction: SELECT the row FOR UPDATE, waiting up to 2 s for a send in flight (absent means 404 MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND); RetryPolicy decides, reading the appointment through booking's AppointmentDirectory; then UPDATE notification_messages SET status='PENDING', attempts=0, next_attempt_at=:now, last_error=NULL, failure_reason=NULL WHERE id=:id RETURNING the row. Any message the policy refuses is returned unchanged with 200. The row stays locked until the commit, so a cancel committing meanwhile still deletes it (RetryMessageApiIT.cancelCommittingWhileARetryHoldsTheMessageStillDeletesIt). The dispatcher picks the row up within 2 s. A recipient ending 0000 fails again by design, which is the point of the demo. A CONFIRMATION of a cancelled appointment can never be resent: it is not retryable, and the claim marks any such PENDING row superseded. Not rate-limited (gated by the branchbook.inbox.enabled setting).

GET /actuator/health, /actuator/health/readiness, /actuator/health/liveness, /actuator/info, /actuator/prometheusHealth (db indicator, no details), probes, build info and Micrometer metrics. Nothing…

Health (the db indicator, with no component details), probes, build info and Micrometer metrics. Nothing else is exposed.

Request

No body.

Response

GET /actuator/health 200 {"status":"UP","groups":["liveness","readiness"]}  /actuator/health/readiness 200 {"status":"UP"} (503 {"status":"OUT_OF_SERVICE"} while db is down)  /actuator/info 200 {"build":{"version":"1.0.0","time":"2026-10-24T09:15:33Z"}}  /actuator/prometheus 200 text/plain: appointments_booked_total{outcome="confirmed"} 4.0  outbox_pending 1.0

Status codes

  • 200 OK
  • 503 Service Unavailable: readiness DOWN/OUT_OF_SERVICE (db indicator failing); liveness stays UP

Notes

management.endpoints.web.exposure.include=health,info,prometheus in application.yml; the hosted demo's task sets MANAGEMENT_ENDPOINTS_WEB_EXPOSURE_INCLUDE=health,info, and its WAF blocks /actuator. Health shows no component details in any environment (ApplicationStartsIT.healthRevealsNoComponentDetails), so there is no outbox indicator: the simulated inbox and the SQL in docs/runbook.md show the outbox (ADR-0003 Notes). Recipients never appear. Docker HEALTHCHECK and compose --wait poll /readyz. permitAll, no auth. Spring Security's Cache-Control: no-store applies here too.

GET /v3/api-docs, /swagger-ui.htmlLive OpenAPI 3.1 document and Swagger UI. The committed docs/openapi.yaml must equal the…

Live OpenAPI 3.1 document and Swagger UI. The committed docs/openapi.yaml must equal the live spec.

Request

No body. /v3/api-docs returns JSON; /v3/api-docs.yaml returns YAML.

Response

200 application/json {"openapi":"3.1.0","info":{"title":"BranchBook API","version":"v1"},"paths":{"/api/v1/branches":{...},"/api/v1/appointments":{...}},"components":{"schemas":{"AppointmentResponse":{...},"Problem":{...}}}}  /swagger-ui.html 200 text/html (redirects to /swagger-ui/index.html)

Status codes

  • 200 OK
  • 302 Found: /swagger-ui.html -> /swagger-ui/index.html

Notes

springdoc-openapi 3.x with springdoc.writer-with-order-by-keys=true, so the output is deterministic. OpenApiContractIT.committedContractMatchesTheRunningApi fetches /v3/api-docs.yaml and compares it exactly with docs/openapi.yaml, with no normalisation code; make contract runs it with -Dopenapi.update=true, which rewrites the committed file, and then regenerates the web types. Two more checks keep the document honest: every parameter pattern is anchored, so a client validating against it accepts exactly what the server does, and real responses, successes and problems alike, have exactly the properties their schemas declare. Only /api/** is documented, every response property is required, and a nullable enum lists null. web: npm run check:api fails when src/api/schema.d.ts is not what openapi-typescript generates from docs/openapi.yaml. Both endpoints are on in every environment, because the API is public. Swagger UI needs inline styles, so /swagger-ui/** alone gets style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' (SecurityHeadersIT.swaggerUiAloneMayUseInlineStyles); inline scripts stay refused there, and the SPA's policy stays strict.

GET /book, /manage, /messages, /privacy, /appointments/{reference}SpaForwardingController serves index.html for client-side routes, so a refresh on /book,…

SpaForwardingController serves index.html for client-side routes, so a refresh on /book, /appointments/APT-..., /manage, /messages or /privacy works.

Request

Only these five routes forward to index.html; any other unknown path is a real 404 problem without a code.

Response

200 text/html index.html (Cache-Control: no-cache). Hashed assets under /assets/* are served from classpath:/static with Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable.

Status codes

  • 200 OK: index.html for app routes; static asset for /assets/*
  • 404 Not Found: any other path (plain Spring problem)

Notes

Single origin: Vite builds into src/main/resources/static, and in dev the Vite proxy forwards /api to :8080. Security headers (CSP default-src 'self', nosniff, frame-ancestors 'none') apply here too. React style props are applied through CSSOM and are not blocked by style-src 'self'; no library may inject <style> elements. A real-browser pass over every screen, on the jar under this policy, found no violations, and from M6 Playwright fails on any securitypolicyviolation event. No server-side rendering.

Error codes

Every code below appears in at least one endpoint contract above and is catalogued in docs/errors.md. No problem body ever carries an appointment reference, a booked time, a name or mobile digits.

INTERNAL_ERROR TRY_AGAIN VALIDATION_FAILED BRANCH_NOT_FOUND SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS SLOT_OFF_GRID SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT CUSTOMER_OVERLAP SLOT_FULL APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED TOO_LATE_TO_CANCEL MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED

DesignBuilt

Business rules and validation

Status. Proven: BR-1 to BR-25. BR-14 (cancellation) and BR-24 (no confirmation after a cancellation) are proven from M6, and BR-17 gained the AWS gateway and the per-recipient daily cap after M6. Since then a cancel deletes the booking from V6 (BR-12, BR-13, BR-14, BR-16, BR-24), the cap counts a ledger of keyed fingerprints from V7 and a refusal the gateway calls permanent fails a message at once (BR-17), and the lookup counts live in PostgreSQL from V8 (BR-13); every rule is still proven. docs/domain-rules.md holds the live status of every rule, and CI checks it.

Every rule has an identifier, the layer that enforces it, and a named test. The same table goes into docs/domain-rules.md, so a reviewer who never runs the code can still see what is proven.

IDRuleEnforced inTest
BR-1Slots start at opens_at + k x service.duration_minutes (15/30/45) in Branch.timezone; a startsAt off that grid is rejected with 422 SLOT_OFF_GRID.domain (SlotCalendar.slotsOn lists the grid for availability; SlotCalendar.validate checks a requested start for booking; SlotCalendarTest proves the two agree)SlotCalendarTest.slotsStartAtOpeningAndStepByServiceDuration
BR-2A slot is offered only if startsAt >= opens_at and startsAt + duration <= closes_at. Worked values: weekday 30-minute last start 16:30 and 45-minute 16:15; Saturday (08:00-13:00) 45-minute last start 11:45; Rustenburg Saturday (08:00-12:00) 45-minute last start 11:00 and 30-minute 11:30. On-grid starts that end after closing are 422 SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS: 17:00 on a 08:00-17:00 day, 12:30 for a 45-minute service on a 13:00 Saturday, 11:45 for a 45-minute service at RUS. ends_at is always computed server-side.domain (SlotCalendar.validate) + database constraint (appointments_ends_after_starts)SlotCalendarTest.saturdayFortyFiveMinuteLastStartIs1145; SlotCalendarTest.rustenburgSaturdayFortyFiveMinuteLastStartIs1100; SlotCalendarTest.onGridStartEndingAfterCloseIsOutsideHours
BR-3No slots on a weekday without an operating-hours row, nor on a public holiday (Schedule 1 or proclaimed date, or the Monday derived after a Sunday holiday; Saturdays get no substitute) unless branch.open_on_public_holidays; a POST on such a day is 422 SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY and availability returns closedReason CLOSED_DAY | PUBLIC_HOLIDAY. Holiday coverage ends 31 Dec of the last seeded year. A WARN, logged at start-up and every day at 06:00 SAST, reports it from 60 days before the window crosses it, and again once the window has.domain (SlotCalendar + PublicHolidayCalendar pure function)PublicHolidayCalendarTest.sundayHoliday9Aug2026IsObservedOnMonday10Aug
BR-4Lead time: startsAt >= clock.now() + PT2H; earlier slots are absent from availability and a stale POST is 422 SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME.domain (SlotCalendar with injected Clock)SlotCalendarTest.excludesSlotsStartingWithinTwoHoursOfNow
BR-5Horizon: the slot date must lie in the half-open window [today, today + P30D) in Branch.timezone, i.e. exactly 30 bookable dates (today through today + 29); availability dates or POST starts outside it return 422 DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW. The server publishes the window as bookableFrom/bookableTo on GET /branches/{code}, so the date strip never derives 'today' from the device.domain (SlotCalendar, BookingWindow) + application service (AvailabilityQueries date check)AvailabilityApiIT.dateOutsideWindowReturns422; BookingWindowTest.thirtyDayHorizonGivesExactlyThirtyBookableDates
BR-6A branch offers a service only if a branch_services row exists; otherwise availability and booking both return 422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH, as they do for an unknown or inactive service code; the composite FK makes it a schema fact.database constraint (appointments_branch_service_fkey) + application service (BranchCalendarProvider.offerFor, used by AvailabilityQueries and, from M3, BookAppointmentHandler step 2)AvailabilityApiIT.serviceNotOfferedAtBranchReturns422
BR-7At most branch_services.capacity active appointments of a service may overlap at any instant; seats are allocated as the lowest free seat under the branch_services row lock; a full slot returns 409 SLOT_FULL with up to three alternatives; a race for the last seat yields exactly one 201 (the 20 racers use 20 distinct synthetic mobiles so only the seat rule is under test).database constraint (appointments_seat_no_overlap) + application service (BookAppointmentHandler FOR UPDATE + generate_series seat query)ConcurrentBookingIT.twentyRacersForOneSeatProduceExactlyOne201
BR-8The seat invariant holds against any code path: a raw-JDBC insert on a taken seat is rejected by the exclusion constraint even with no lock held.database constraint (appointments_seat_no_overlap, btree_gist)SchemaConstraintIT.overlappingSeatIsRejectedEvenWithoutTheApplication
BR-9One person, one place: a customer_mobile cannot hold two active appointments whose [starts_at, ends_at) overlap, at any branch or service; violation returns 409 CUSTOMER_OVERLAP. Decided by a pre-check under the per-mobile advisory lock; appointments_customer_no_overlap is the backstop for any path that skips the lock.application service (BookAppointmentHandler steps 4-5) + database constraint (appointments_customer_no_overlap) mapped by ConstraintViolationTranslatorBookAppointmentIT.overlappingAppointmentAtAnotherBranchReturns409CustomerOverlap
BR-10At most one upcoming active appointment per (customer_mobile, service): a second booking while an earlier one is CONFIRMED or CHECKED_IN and ends_at > now returns 409 DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT; cancelled, completed, no-show and past rows never count. Checked under the per-mobile advisory lock (a partial unique index cannot express 'upcoming' because index predicates must be immutable). The problem body carries no reference, branch or time.application service (BookAppointmentHandler step 5, under the step 4 pg_advisory_xact_lock)BookAppointmentIT.sameMobileSameServiceSecondBookingReturns409WithoutRevealingReference
BR-11Every appointment gets APT-XXXX-XXXX from the 31-character alphabet via SecureRandom, stored uppercase and unique; lookup input is normalised; a collision re-runs the booking transaction once.domain (AppointmentReference) + database constraint (appointments_reference_key)AppointmentReferenceTest.usesOnlyUnambiguousAlphabet
BR-12POST /api/v1/appointments requires Idempotency-Key (UUID); the key row is the first statement of the transaction; same key + same hash replays 201 with Idempotent-Replayed: true and a body re-rendered from the current appointment; same key + different hash is 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED; a failed booking leaves no key row; a key is honoured for exactly 24 h, after which it books anew whether or not the hourly purge has removed it; once the booking a key made is cancelled, and so deleted (BR-14), the key keeps response_status 201 with no appointment until its 24 hours run out, and the same key with the same details is 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED with a fixed detail: it never books again.database constraint (idempotency_keys_pkey, claimed with INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE, which takes over only a key whose 24 hours have run out; idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey ON DELETE SET NULL and idempotency_keys_completed_consistent, from V6, so a completed key outlives its cancelled appointment) + application service (BookAppointmentHandler step 1, JdbcIdempotencyKeyStore)IdempotencyIT.concurrentSameKey_bookOnceAndBothReceiveSameReference; IdempotencyIT.sameKeyAfterItsBookingIsCancelledReturns422AndNeverBooksAgain
BR-13Viewing or cancelling requires the reference plus X-Mobile-Last4; any mismatch, including an unknown reference, is 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND (never 403), and a cancelled reference is unknown, because the cancel deleted its appointment (BR-14), so a later lookup or cancel of it is that same 404 and is counted; a missing or malformed header is 400 VALIDATION_FAILED and not counted; five wrong lookups of a reference block it, right digits included, with 429 LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED and Retry-After until an hour after the first; a lookup counts as a try from before the database read until its digits are compared, so lookups that arrive together cannot pass the limit, and a burst of right ones may be refused for a second (Retry-After: 1).application service (AppointmentAccess, the one check LookupAppointmentHandler and CancelAppointmentHandler share, + LookupAttemptLimiter, each count a short transaction of its own over JdbcLookupAttemptStore, which locks the reference's row; a lookup in flight holds its place for a 30-second lease at most; LookupAttemptPurgeJob deletes closed windows hourly) + database (lookup_attempts, V8, one row per reference with an open window, shared by every instance)AppointmentApiIT.getWithWrongLast4Returns404NotForbidden; LookupAttemptLimiterIT.countsAreSharedBetweenInstances; CancelAppointmentIT.lookupAndRepeatCancelAfterACancelReturn404AndAreCounted
BR-14Cancellation: CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED only while now < starts_at (else 409 TOO_LATE_TO_CANCEL, and the appointment is left as it was). From V6 the cancel deletes the booking at once, in one transaction: it locks the appointment's row with its version checked, deletes every CONFIRMATION of it whatever its status, writes the PENDING CANCELLATION SMS (unless the daily cap stops it, BR-17), and deletes the appointment with its version checked, which frees the seat. Only the cancellation SMS, until it is final (BR-17), and the idempotency key, until its 24 hours run out (BR-12), are kept, with no appointment. The 200 response shows the appointment as the cancel left it, with only the cancellation SMS. From the commit the reference is unknown: a lookup or a repeat cancel is 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND, counted (BR-13). Of two concurrent cancels one answers 200 and the other that counted 404, as does a cancel whose appointment the retention purge deleted; never a 500. A cancel that waits longer than lock_timeout, such as on a confirmation being sent, is 503 TRY_AGAIN and changes nothing.domain (Appointment.cancel guard) + application service (CancelAppointmentHandler; JpaAppointmentRepository.lockForDeletion and delete, each with the version) + database constraint (notification_messages_appointment_fkey and idempotency_keys_appointment_fkey ON DELETE SET NULL; notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only, which fails the delete if a confirmation were left behind)CancelAppointmentIT.cancelFreesSeatForNewBooking; CancelAppointmentIT.cancelReturnsTheCancelledAppointmentWithOnlyItsCancellationAndDeletesIt; CancelAppointmentIT.twoConcurrentCancelsGiveOne200AndOne404ThatIsCounted
BR-15AppointmentStatus has exactly five states with four legal edges (CONFIRMED->CANCELLED|CHECKED_IN|NO_SHOW, CHECKED_IN->COMPLETED); terminal states reject every transition; only CONFIRMED->CANCELLED is reachable through the API; the Java ACTIVE set equals the schema predicate and ACTIVE_SQL is its only spelling in native SQL.domain (AppointmentStatus.canTransitionTo) + database constraint (status CHECK, timestamp-consistency CHECKs, partial predicates)AppointmentStatusTest.terminalStatesRejectEveryTransition
BR-16Every booking writes its PENDING CONFIRMATION rows (SMS always; EMAIL only if the Tier 3 email channel ships and an address was given) and every cancellation its CANCELLATION row by direct call inside the same transaction, which also deletes the appointment's confirmations and then the appointment (BR-14); where the daily cap stops an SMS, the confirmation is written FAILED instead and the cancellation not at all (BR-17); a rolled-back booking or cancel leaves no change; appointment status never depends on delivery status.application service (BookAppointmentHandler step 9 -> NotificationOutbox.enqueueConfirmation; CancelAppointmentHandler step 6 -> NotificationOutbox.enqueueCancellation)ConfirmationOutboxIT.bookingAndPendingMessagesCommitAtomically
BR-17Delivery: NotificationDispatcher runs every 2 s on its own single thread, outbox-1, owned by DispatcherLoop (isolation from the default scheduler, not throughput) and processes up to 20 due PENDING rows per tick, one transaction per row: claim with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT 1, send, mark with an explicit UPDATE, commit. Failures retry after 2, 4, 8 and 16 s (bound from the Clock); the fifth failed attempt marks FAILED with failure_reason DELIVERY_FAILED (30 s of backoff, about 35 s end to end with the 2 s ticks). A refusal the gateway calls permanent, which sending again cannot cure, marks FAILED with DELIVERY_FAILED on that attempt; a gateway that throws (last_error GATEWAY_ERROR) and the simulated refusal of a number ending 0000 are retryable. last_error holds the refusal's fixed code, which the outbox.retry and outbox.failed log lines give too. A row whose mark fails is set FAILED (failure_reason DISPATCH_ERROR, last_error 'MARK_FAILED') in a separate transaction, so a poisoned row is sent at most once more and never loops. A CANCELLATION, which outlives its deleted appointment (BR-14), is kept only until it is final: where a confirmation would be marked SENT or FAILED, it is deleted instead, in the same transaction, and logged as outbox.deleted. FAILED is replayable via POST /api/v1/messages/{id}/retry (from M6, while the inbox is switched on) or the guarded runbook SQL. The gateway is the simulated one unless branchbook.sms.gateway is aws; then every number outside the published demo block (082 555 0000 to 0199, always simulated) is sent through AWS End User Messaging SMS without SDK retries, and each refusal is a fixed AWS_* code from the exception's type and typed reason: permanent for any ConflictException (such as a number the SMS sandbox has not verified), AccessDeniedException, ValidationException, ResourceNotFoundException and a monthly spend limit reached, so FAILED at once; retryable for any other, with the same backoff and fifth-attempt FAILED. Where branchbook.sms.recipient-daily-cap is above 0 (off by default), a message whose number was written that many SMS in the last 24 hours, counted in the ledger sms_recipient_sends (V7) by HMAC-SHA256 of the number under branchbook.sms.recipient-cap-key, so the messages a cancel or the dispatcher deleted still count, is FAILED with RECIPIENT_DAILY_CAP and never sent (a capped confirmation is stored, a capped cancellation only reported in the cancel's response), and the booking or cancel still succeeds.application service (NotificationDispatcher.runOnce, driven by DispatcherLoop; the MessageGateway port, built by SmsGatewayConfiguration: SimulatedSmsGateway, or RoutingSmsGateway over SimulatedSmsGateway and AwsSmsGateway; NotificationOutbox counts the cap through RecipientSendLedger, by RecipientFingerprints, and RecipientSendLedgerPurgeJob deletes rows 24 hours old, hourly) + database (partial index ix_notification_messages_due; table sms_recipient_sends with ix_sms_recipient_sends_fingerprint and ix_sms_recipient_sends_written, V7, which drops ix_notification_messages_recipient; row locks)NotificationDispatcherIT.retriesWithBackoffThenMarksFailedAfterFiveAttempts; RoutingSmsGatewayTest.theDemoBlockIsAlwaysSimulated; RecipientDailyCapIT.theSmsPastTheCapIsWrittenFailedAndTheBookingStillSucceeds; NotificationDispatcherIT.permanentRefusalMarksFailedOnItsFirstAttemptAndLogsItsCode; RecipientCapLedgerIT.bookingAndCancellingInTurnReachesTheCapThoughEveryMessageIsDeleted
BR-18The confirmation SMS fits one GSM-7 segment (<= 160 septets, GSM-7 charset only) for every seeded branch/service combination and for the configured public base URL (worst case from the column bounds: 87 fixed characters + 18 + 20 + the /manage URL, so the base URL may be at most 28 characters; longer values fail start-up), states service, branch, SAST time, reference and the /manage link, formats day and month names with Locale.ENGLISH, and never contains the full mobile, its last four digits or an ID number. A template regression can never fail a booking: the database bound is a coarse 320-character guard.domain (NotificationTemplates, Gsm7) + start-up check (NotificationProperties computes NotificationTemplates.longestConfirmationSeptets for the configured base URL) + database constraint (services.sms_name <= 18, branches.sms_name <= 20, GSM-7-safe sms_name CHECKs, notification_messages_sms_len)NotificationTemplatesTest.confirmationFitsOneGsm7SegmentForLongestSeededNames; NotificationPropertiesTest.rejectsBaseUrlThatOverflowsSms; NotificationTemplatesTest.rendersEnglishDayNamesUnderAnyDefaultLocale
BR-19A South African mobile number is mandatory, accepted in the six documented formats, parsed only when libphonenumber reports MOBILE and the E.164 form matches the schema CHECK pattern, stored only as E.164, displayed 082 555 0101, masked 082 *** 0101 only to a caller who proved the number, redacted 082 *** **** in the inbox and every other surface; logs, MDC, the PostgreSQL log and exception messages carry no name, email, number or masked last four digits, and log lines name an appointment by its id, never its reference.domain (MobileNumber, CustomerName, AppointmentReference: each toString is redacted) + database constraint (customer_mobile CHECK; log_error_verbosity=terse) + driver (pgjdbc logServerErrorDetail=false) + configuration (Hibernate's SQL error log, org.hibernate.orm.jdbc.error, off) + application service (ProblemDetailHandler never echoes input; log lines carry ids, codes and constraint names only, never a reference; AwsSmsGateway stores fixed codes, never AWS's exception message, which can quote the number)MobileNumberTest.sixInputFormatsNormaliseToPlus27825550101; LogRedactionIT.bookingLogsContainNoCustomerNameOrMobile
BR-20Time: every instant is stored as timestamptz (UTC), slots are computed in Branch.timezone through an injected Clock, application SQL never calls now() (every time-dependent statement binds :now from the Clock; DEFAULT now() is a raw-insert convenience only), the API emits ISO-8601 with the branch offset (+02:00) through one explicit formatter, the UI formats with Intl timeZone Africa/Johannesburg and prints SAST, and the JVM and containers run TZ=UTC while CI also runs the API's unit and API tests under TZ=America/New_York, so slot output is proven identical under any default zone.domain (SlotCalendar) + application service (explicit ISO_OFFSET_DATE_TIME rendering at Branch.timezone, independent of Jackson 2/3 time-zone features; :now bound in every native statement) + UI (web/src/lib/time.ts, Intl with Africa/Johannesburg)AvailabilityApiIT.rendersStartsAtWithJohannesburgOffset; SlotCalendarTest.producesSameSlotsUnderAnyJvmDefaultZone; NativeSqlUsesBoundNowTest.noJavaSqlLiteralCallsNow; time.test.ts
BR-21SlotCalendar.validate returns exactly one code when several apply, by fixed precedence: DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW, SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY, SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS, SLOT_OFF_GRID, SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME (the window and the day decide whether a grid exists at all; lead time is last because it is the only one that changes as a page goes stale).domain (SlotCalendar.validate)SlotCalendarTest.validatePrecedence
BR-22Bookings by one mobile are serialised by pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(mobileE164, 0)), taken after the idempotency row and before the branch_services row (fixed lock order: idempotency key, mobile, branch_services), and conflicts are reported in a fixed order: DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT, then CUSTOMER_OVERLAP, then SLOT_FULL. Two concurrent bookings by one mobile at different branches therefore give one 201 and one 409, never a deadlock; 40P01 and 40001 from any path that bypasses the order re-run the transaction once, then 503 TRY_AGAIN.application service (BookAppointmentHandler steps 4-5) + database (advisory lock, row locks)ConcurrentBookingIT.sameMobileTwoBranchesOverlappingConcurrentlyGivesOne201AndOne409CustomerOverlap
BR-23No problem body reveals another appointment: 409 DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT and 409 CUSTOMER_OVERLAP carry neither reference nor branch nor time, so a caller who knows only a cellphone number learns no credential. The residual signal (an appointment exists for that number) is an accepted demo risk closed in production by OTP-verified identity (ADR-0004).application service (ProblemDetailHandler, ConstraintViolationTranslator)AppointmentApiIT.noProblemBodyEverContainsAnAppointmentReference
BR-24A confirmation is never delivered after its appointment's cancellation: from V6 enqueueCancellation deletes every CONFIRMATION row of the appointment, whatever its status, in the cancel transaction, before the appointment is deleted (waiting at most lock_timeout for a send in flight), so a confirmation is delivered before its cancellation or never; the dispatcher marks FAILED with failure_reason SUPERSEDED_BY_CANCELLATION a confirmation whose appointment it finds cancelled (one cancelled before V6, or by hand); and retry refuses CONFIRMATION rows of a cancelled or started appointment (200 no-op returning the row unchanged).application service (NotificationOutbox.enqueueCancellation; NotificationDispatcher, which asks booking's AppointmentDirectory before it sends a confirmation and supersedes one whose appointment is cancelled; RetryPolicy, the one rule behind RetryMessageHandler and the inbox's retryable) + database (row locks; notification_messages_detached_cancellation_only: no confirmation outlives its appointment)NotificationDispatcherIT.cancelDeletesThePendingConfirmationAndTheSentCancellationIsDeleted; CancelAppointmentIT.cancelDeletesTheConfirmationWhetherPendingFailedOrSent
BR-25Past appointments never trap the customer: a CONFIRMED appointment whose time has passed stays CONFIRMED in v1 (no staff actor), is not cancellable (canCancel false, 409 TOO_LATE_TO_CANCEL), and does not block a new booking of the same service, because every customer rule compares against the Clock rather than stored state.application service (BookAppointmentHandler step 5: ends_at > :now) + domain (Appointment.canCancel(now))BookAppointmentIT.pastConfirmedAppointmentDoesNotBlockSameServiceBooking

Validation rules

FieldRuleMessage the customer sees
customer.firstNameRequired; normalised to NFC; U+2019, U+2018 and U+02BC mapped to the ASCII apostrophe (iOS Smart Punctuation); trimmed; internal whitespace collapsed; 1-60 code points; ^\p{L}[\p{L}\p{M} '\-]*$ (letters, combining marks, space, hyphen, apostrophe). Same normalisation and zod rule client-side; server returns 400 VALIDATION_FAILED errors[{field:'customer.firstName', code:'NAME_INVALID'}]; the normalised value is what is hashed and stored.Enter your first name using letters, spaces, hyphens or apostrophes (up to 60 characters).
customer.lastNameSame as firstName (required, NFC, curly apostrophes mapped, 1-60 code points, letters/marks/space/hyphen/apostrophe); code NAME_INVALID.Enter your last name using letters, spaces, hyphens or apostrophes (up to 60 characters).
customer.mobileRequired. Client pre-check: strip spaces, hyphens, dots, brackets and '(0)', then match ^(\+27|0027|27|0)(6[0-9]|7[0-9]|8[1-5])[0-9]{7}$. Server: MobileNumber.parse via libphonenumber region ZA, accepted only when the type is MOBILE and the E.164 form matches E164_PATTERN ^\+27(6[0-9]|7[0-9]|8[1-5])[0-9]{7}$ ('082 555 0101', '0825550101', '+27 82 555 0101', '+27 (0)82 555 0101', '27825550101', '0027825550101' -> +27825550101); landlines 01-05, VoIP 087 (including sub-ranges libphonenumber labels MOBILE), 080/086 rejected; stored E.164 only under a CHECK that uses the same pattern, so a parsed number can never raise 23514. 422 MOBILE_NUMBER_REQUIRED, errors[{field:'customer.mobile'}]. Where branchbook.booking.demo-numbers-only is on (off by default, and off on the hosted demo since it sends real SMS), a South African mobile outside 082 555 0000 to 0199 is 422 DEMO_NUMBER_REQUIRED before anything is written.We need a cellphone number to SMS your confirmation.SA
customer.emailExists only if the Tier 3 email channel ships. Optional; blank -> null; trimmed and lower-cased (Locale.ROOT); RFC 5322 shape (Hibernate @Email, zod .email()); <= 254 characters; when present an EMAIL confirmation is queued; rendered n***@example.com outside proved responses. Code EMAIL_INVALID inside 400 VALIDATION_FAILED.Enter a valid email address, or leave this blank.
branchCodeRequired; ^[A-Za-z]{2,8}$, upper-cased before use (400 VALIDATION_FAILED otherwise); must match an active branch, else 404 BRANCH_NOT_FOUND. The UI only offers codes from GET /branches.We couldn't find that branch.
serviceCodeRequired; ^[A-Za-z]{2,12}$, upper-cased before use (400 otherwise); a branch_services row for (branch, service) must exist, else 422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH (also the composite FK). The UI only offers services from GET /branches/{code}.This branch doesn't offer that service.
startsAt (format)Required; strict ISO-8601 OffsetDateTime with an explicit offset, e.g. 2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00; converted to Instant server-side; endsAt in the body is rejected as an unknown field. 400 VALIDATION_FAILED.startsAt must be an ISO-8601 date-time with an offset, for example 2026-10-27T09:30:00+02:00.SA
startsAt (precedence)When several slot rules fail at once, SlotCalendar.validate reports only the first in this order: DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW, SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY, SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS, SLOT_OFF_GRID, SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME (SlotCalendarTest.validatePrecedence). Example: 12:15 on a Saturday for a 45-minute service is inside hours (ends 13:00) but off the 08:00 grid -> SLOT_OFF_GRID; 06:00 on a weekday -> SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS, not SLOT_OFF_GRID.(the message of the first applicable code below)
startsAt (grid)In Branch.timezone, (startsAt - opens_at) must be a multiple of service.duration_minutes; 422 SLOT_OFF_GRID.Choose one of the times shown.SA
startsAt (hours)startsAt >= opens_at and startsAt + duration <= closes_at for that weekday in Branch.timezone; 422 SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS (e.g. 12:30 for a 45-minute service on a 13:00 Saturday; 11:45 for a 45-minute service at RUS).The branch is closed at that time. Choose a time during opening hours.SA
startsAt (open day)The date must have an operating-hours row and must not be a public holiday (scheduled, proclaimed or derived Monday) unless open_on_public_holidays; 422 SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY. The date strip shows the reason ('Closed on Sundays' / 'Public holiday: Heritage Day').The branch is closed on that day.SA
startsAt (lead time)startsAt >= clock.now() + PT2H evaluated in Africa/Johannesburg; 422 SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME. The UI refetches availability and keeps the customer's details.That time is too soon. Please choose a later slot.SA
startsAt / date (horizon)Date in the half-open window [today, today + 30 days) in Branch.timezone: 30 bookable dates, today through today + 29, published by the server as bookableFrom/bookableTo; applies to POST startsAt and to GET availability ?date=; 422 DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW.You can book up to 30 days ahead. Choose an earlier date.SA
startsAt (seat available)A free seat must exist under the branch_services lock; otherwise 409 SLOT_FULL with alternatives[] (up to three later slots the same day with seatsAvailable > 0). UI: on the review step, 'That time was just taken' with the alternatives as buttons and a link to all times; availability refetched, details kept, a fresh Idempotency-Key minted.That time was just taken, followed by the alternatives.
customer.mobile + serviceCode (duplicate)No upcoming active appointment (CONFIRMED or CHECKED_IN with ends_at > now) may exist for the same mobile and service; checked under the per-mobile advisory lock before the seat is considered -> 409 DUPLICATE_APPOINTMENT. The body carries no reference, branch or time (no existingReference): the customer's own reference is in their SMS. Past, cancelled, completed and no-show rows never count.You already have an upcoming appointment for this service.
customer.mobile + startsAt (overlap)No active appointment for the same mobile may overlap [startsAt, endsAt) at any branch; checked under the per-mobile lock after the duplicate rule, with appointments_customer_no_overlap as the database backstop -> 409 CUSTOMER_OVERLAP. The body carries no reference, branch or time.You already have an appointment at that time.
Idempotency-Key (header)Required on POST /api/v1/appointments; must parse as a UUID; 400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED. The UI always sends one (useIdempotencyKey, crypto.randomUUID), so this message is seen by API callers only.Idempotency-Key header is required and must be a UUID.
Idempotency-Key (reuse)Same key with a different SHA-256 request hash -> 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED; same key + same hash -> 201 with Idempotent-Replayed: true and the current representation of the booked appointment (no error). A key stays bound to its booking for exactly 24 h, purged or not; after that the same key books anew. Once its booking is cancelled, and so deleted, the same key and hash -> 422 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED with a fixed detail, never a new booking.None in the app, which mints a new key whenever the details change; API callers get: This Idempotency-Key was already used with different details. Send a new key for a new booking. After a cancel: The booking this Idempotency-Key made has been cancelled. Send a new key to book again.
reference (path and LookupForm)Trim, upper-case, accept missing hyphens, then must match ^APT-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJKMNP-Z2-9]{4}$; anything else is 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND without a database query and without a limiter entry. Client zod: same normalisation and regex.Enter your reference, for example APT-7K3M-9Q2X.
X-Mobile-Last4 (header) / last4 (LookupForm)Exactly four digits ^[0-9]{4}$. A missing or malformed header is 400 VALIDATION_FAILED (errors[{field:'X-Mobile-Last4'}]) and does not count toward the limiter: a shape check depends only on the request, never on the appointment, so it reveals nothing. The UI validates shape before calling.Enter the last 4 digits of the cellphone number used to book.
reference + X-Mobile-Last4 (match)Reference must exist and last4 must equal MobileNumber.last4() (MessageDigest.isEqual, in constant time); unknown reference and wrong digits are both 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND with an identical body; each failure counts toward the per-reference limiter.We couldn't find an appointment with those details.
lookup attempts (rate limit)Five wrong lookups of a reference within an hour of the first block it: every later lookup, right digits included, is 429 LOOKUP_RATE_LIMITED with Retry-After (seconds until the window closes). One row per normalised reference in lookup_attempts (V8), shared by every instance and kept across restarts, with a fixed window that opens at the first failure and closes one hour later (the row holds its own closing time; LookupAttemptPurgeJob deletes closed windows hourly, so sprayed unknown references leave about an hour of rows). Each lookup counts as in flight from before the database read until its digits are compared; right ones are then released, so 2 s confirmation polling never leads to the hour-long block, and a lookup refused only because failures and lookups in flight together reach 5 gets Retry-After: 1.Too many attempts. Try again later.
cancel (timing)POST .../cancel allowed only while clock.now() < starts_at; at or after starts_at -> 409 TOO_LATE_TO_CANCEL; already cancelled, and so deleted -> 404 APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND, counted.This appointment has already started.SA
availability ?date= (format)Required; ISO yyyy-MM-dd; interpreted in Branch.timezone; 400 VALIDATION_FAILED on bad format; window check per the horizon rule.date must be in the form YYYY-MM-DD.SA
availability ?service=Required; ^[A-Za-z]{2,12}$, upper-cased before use; must be offered at the branch (422 SERVICE_NOT_OFFERED_AT_BRANCH, also for an unknown or inactive code).This branch doesn't offer that service.
messages ?limit=Optional integer, default 50, 1..200; outside the range 400 VALIDATION_FAILED; endpoint exists only while branchbook.inbox.enabled is true (off by default, then a plain 404). Every row's recipientMasked is redacted (082 *** ****, n***@example.com): the inbox never shows a last-four factor next to a reference. Where branchbook.inbox.redact-references is on, every reference, in the field and in the text, reads APT-****-****.limit must be between 1 and 200.
messages/{id}/retryid must be a UUID (400 VALIDATION_FAILED otherwise); unknown id, including a cancellation deleted once final -> 404 MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND; a retryable row (FAILED with failure_reason DELIVERY_FAILED or DISPATCH_ERROR, and not a CONFIRMATION whose appointment is CANCELLED or has started) becomes PENDING with attempts 0, next_attempt_at :now and last_error and failure_reason NULL; any other row (PENDING, SENT, superseded or for a past slot) is a 200 no-op returning the row unchanged. The runbook SQL fallback carries the same guard.We couldn't find that message.
X-Correlation-Id (header)Optional; accepted if <= 64 characters matching ^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$, otherwise silently replaced by a generated UUID; echoed on every response and in every ProblemDetail; copied to notification_messages.correlation_id ('sys-' + UUID for work outside a request); no error is ever returned for it.(none; value replaced)
request body (shape)Strict JSON: unknown properties rejected (FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES) and malformed JSON -> 400 VALIDATION_FAILED; for an unknown property errors[] carries field = the property path only when it matches ^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9.]{0,60}$ (else '(body)') and code UNKNOWN_FIELD; submitted values are never echoed.This field is not allowed.
request body (size)Not enforced in the app in v1: the delivery plan cuts the request-body size filter (no 413 PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE), and body-size limits belong to the gateway/WAF in production; Tomcat maxPostSize applies only to form posts and is not relied on.The request is too large.
idNumber (NOT collected in v1)Deliberately not a form field (POPIA s10 minimality; identity verified in branch). Documented in docs/production-notes.md for FICA-style services: 13 digits YYMMDDSSSSCAZ; YYMMDD a real calendar date (century inferred, 000230 rejected, 000229 accepted); C in {0,1}; Luhn mod-10 over all 13 digits; derived age >= 18; synthetic example 9001015000085; never echoed in errors or logs; would be stored AES-GCM with an HMAC index column.Enter a valid 13-digit South African ID number.SA

DesignBuilt

South African specifics

Status. Built: public holidays for 2026 and 2027 with the Sunday-to-Monday rule and the coverage warning, per-branch trading hours, slots computed in the branch's zone and written with +02:00, South African mobile numbers stored as +27 E.164, every time shown in SAST, and the POPIA notice; after M6, real SMS to South African numbers only, sent from af-south-1, and the hosted demo's deletion of each booking once its appointment is over, with backups kept 7 days; and, in every deployment, a cancel's deletion of the booking at once, all but its cancellation SMS, kept until it is sent or has failed for good. Not built: de-identification and a bank's retention schedule, which are production notes.

These rules are what make the system read as built for South Africa rather than ported from a tutorial. Each one is data or a pure function with dated tests.

Public holidays, 2026 and 2027

Public Holidays Act 36 of 1994, s2(1): "The days mentioned in Schedule 1 shall be public holidays, and whenever any public holiday falls on a Sunday, the following Monday shall be a public holiday." Saturdays get no substitute. The Labour Appeal Court (Randfontein Estates v NUM) held the Monday is an additional holiday, not a replacement, so both Sunday and Monday are holidays. s2A lets the President declare extra once-off holidays by proclamation (used for 27 Dec 2022 and 4 Nov 2026). Design implication: store a public_holidays table (date, name, source) seeded from gov.za plus proclamations, and PublicHolidayCalendar.holidayOn(date) derives the observed Monday that adds the Monday after any Sunday holiday; never hard-code dates in business logic and never derive Saturday substitutes.

How the calendar rules are enforced

IDRuleTest
BR-1Slots start at opens_at + k x service.duration_minutes (15/30/45) in Branch.timezone; a startsAt off that grid is rejected with 422 SLOT_OFF_GRID.SlotCalendarTest.slotsStartAtOpeningAndStepByServiceDuration
BR-2A slot is offered only if startsAt >= opens_at and startsAt + duration <= closes_at. Worked values: weekday 30-minute last start 16:30 and 45-minute 16:15; Saturday (08:00-13:00) 45-minute last start 11:45; Rustenburg Saturday (08:00-12:00) 45-minute last start 11:00 and 30-minute 11:30. On-grid starts that end after closing are 422 SLOT_OUTSIDE_HOURS: 17:00 on a 08:00-17:00 day, 12:30 for a 45-minute service on a 13:00 Saturday, 11:45 for a 45-minute service at RUS. ends_at is always computed server-side.SlotCalendarTest.saturdayFortyFiveMinuteLastStartIs1145; SlotCalendarTest.rustenburgSaturdayFortyFiveMinuteLastStartIs1100; SlotCalendarTest.onGridStartEndingAfterCloseIsOutsideHours
BR-3No slots on a weekday without an operating-hours row, nor on a public holiday (Schedule 1 or proclaimed date, or the Monday derived after a Sunday holiday; Saturdays get no substitute) unless branch.open_on_public_holidays; a POST on such a day is 422 SLOT_ON_CLOSED_DAY and availability returns closedReason CLOSED_DAY | PUBLIC_HOLIDAY. Holiday coverage ends 31 Dec of the last seeded year. A WARN, logged at start-up and every day at 06:00 SAST, reports it from 60 days before the window crosses it, and again once the window has.PublicHolidayCalendarTest.sundayHoliday9Aug2026IsObservedOnMonday10Aug
BR-4Lead time: startsAt >= clock.now() + PT2H; earlier slots are absent from availability and a stale POST is 422 SLOT_WITHIN_LEAD_TIME.SlotCalendarTest.excludesSlotsStartingWithinTwoHoursOfNow
BR-5Horizon: the slot date must lie in the half-open window [today, today + P30D) in Branch.timezone, i.e. exactly 30 bookable dates (today through today + 29); availability dates or POST starts outside it return 422 DATE_OUTSIDE_BOOKING_WINDOW. The server publishes the window as bookableFrom/bookableTo on GET /branches/{code}, so the date strip never derives 'today' from the device.AvailabilityApiIT.dateOutsideWindowReturns422; BookingWindowTest.thirtyDayHorizonGivesExactlyThirtyBookableDates

Branch hours

DayOpensClosesBasis
Monday-Friday08:0017:00Representative of retail-bank branches in South African shopping centres. Some branches extend their hours at month-end, for example to 17:30 from the 25th to the 1st: a good optional 'hours override' feature, not a core requirement.
Saturday08:0013:00Representative. Short day: on its grid from 08:00, a 45-minute service starts last at 11:45 (BR-2).
Sunday09:0013:00Only a minority of branches trade on Sundays, typically 09:00 to 13:00. Model Sunday as closed by default with per-branch rows that open it; seed 2 of 6 branches open on Sunday.
Public holidaysclosedclosedBranches are assumed closed on public holidays. Model as closed by default (branches.open_on_public_holidays = false) so the rule is explicit and overridable rather than hard-coded.
Model note--Hours differ per branch (some mall branches run 09:00-18:00), so operating hours must be a per-branch, per-weekday table (branch_operating_hours), never constants. The README states that the hours are representative and configurable.
